   43: const ProductDetailPage = () => {
   44:   const { id: paramId } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
   45:   const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
   46:   const navigate = useNavigate();

   49:   const fromSearch = location.state?.fromSearch;

   51:   const currentCategory = searchParams.get("cat") || "";
   52:   const id = searchParams.get("id") || paramId;
   53: 
   54:   const productCategory = currentCategory || "todas";
   55: 
   56:   const { data: products = [], isLoading: loading } = useProducts(
   57:     productCategory as any,
   58:   );
   59: 
   60:   const [cartOpen, setCartOpen] = useState(false);
   61:   const [addModalOpen, setAddModalOpen] = useState(false);
   62:   const [selectedRelated, setSelectedRelated] = useState<Product | null>(null);
   63:   const [zoomGallery, setZoomGallery] = useState<{
   64:     initialIndex: number;
   65:   } | null>(null);
   66: 
   67:   const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
   68:   const [qtyInput, setQtyInput] = useState("1");
   69:   const [lastTier, setLastTier] = useState(1);
   70:   const [showUnlock, setShowUnlock] = useState(false);
   71:   const [pricePulse, setPricePulse] = useState(false);
   72:   const [pageReady, setPageReady] = useState(false);
   73: 
   74:   const viewers = useProductViewers();
   75: 
   76:   const {
   77:     cart,
   78:     addToCart,
   79:     removeFromCart,
   80:     changeQty,
   81:     setExactQty,
   82:     setItemNote,
   83:     totalItems,
   84:     totalPrice,
   85:     savings,
   86:   } = useCartStore();
   87: 
   88:   const product = useMemo(
   89:     () => products.find((item) => item.id === id),
   90:     [products, id],
   91:   );
   92: 
   93:   const productMedia = product ? getProductMedia(product) : [];
   94:   const productSeo = getProductSeo(product, id);
   95: 
   96:   const selectedRelatedQty = selectedRelated
   97:     ? (cart.find((i) => i.id === selectedRelated.id)?.qty ?? 0)
   98:     : 0;
   99: 
  100:   useEffect(() => {
  101:     setPageReady(false);
  102: 
  103:     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
  104:       window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
  105:     }, 0);
  106: 
  107:     const readyTimer = window.setTimeout(() => setPageReady(true), 220);
  108: 
  109:     setQty(1);
  110:     setQtyInput("1");
  111:     setLastTier(1);
  112:     setShowUnlock(false);
  113:     setPricePulse(false);
  114: 
  115:     return () => {
  116:       window.clearTimeout(timer);
  117:       window.clearTimeout(readyTimer);
  118:     };
  119:   }, [id]);
  120: 
  121:   const status = (product?.status || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  122:   const available = !!product && ["publicado", "preventa"].includes(status);
  123:   const isPreventa = status === "preventa";
  124:   const isOutOfStock =
  125:     !!product && !isPreventa && !!product.price_1 && product.stock === 0;
  126:   const showWhatsAppButton = isPreventa || isOutOfStock;
  127: 
  128:   const parsedQtyInput =
  129:     qtyInput.trim() !== "" && /^\d+$/.test(qtyInput)
  130:       ? parseInt(qtyInput, 10)
  131:       : null;
  132: 
  133:   const isQtyInputValid = parsedQtyInput !== null && parsedQtyInput >= 1;
  134:   const effectiveQty = isQtyInputValid ? parsedQtyInput : qty;
  135: 
  136:   const unitPrice = product ? getUnitPrice(effectiveQty, product) : 0;
  137:   const total = unitPrice * effectiveQty;
  138:   const nextTier = product ? getNextTier(effectiveQty, product) : null;
  139: 
  140:   const savingsByQty =
  141:     product && product.price_1 > unitPrice
  142:       ? (product.price_1 - unitPrice) * effectiveQty
  143:       : 0;
  144: 
  145:   const related = useRelatedProducts(products, product);
  146:   const stockPresentation = product
  147:     ? getStockPresentation(product, isPreventa)
  148:     : null;
  149: 
  150:   const handleBack = useCallback(() => {
  151:     if (fromSearch) {
  152:       navigate("/catalogo", { state: { restoreSearch: searchQuery } });
  153:       return;
  154:     }
  155: 
  156:     navigate(
  157:       currentCategory
  158:         ? `/catalogo/categoria.html?cat=${encodeURIComponent(currentCategory)}`
  159:         : "/catalogo",
  160:     );
  161:   }, [navigate, currentCategory, fromSearch, searchQuery]);
  162: 
  163:   useEffect(() => {
  164:     const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
  165:       const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
  166:       const isTyping =
  167:         ["INPUT", "TEXTAREA"].includes(target.tagName) ||
  168:         target.isContentEditable;
  169: 
  170:       if (isTyping) return;
  171: 
  172:       if (event.key === "Backspace") {
  173:         event.preventDefault();
  174:         handleBack();
  175:       }
  176:     };
  177: 
  178:     window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  179:     return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  180:   }, [handleBack]);
  181: 
  182:   const handleShare = useCallback(async () => {
  183:     const url = window.location.href;
  184: 
  185:     try {
  186:       if (navigator.share) {
  187:         await navigator.share({
  188:           title: product?.title,
  189:           text: product?.description,
  190:           url,
  191:         });
  192:         return;
  193:       }
  194: 
  195:       await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
  196:     } catch {
  197:       console.warn("No se pudo compartir el producto");
  198:     }
  199:   }, [product]);
  200: 
  201:   const updateQty = useCallback(
  202:     (nextQty: number) => {
  203:       const cleanQty = Math.max(1, nextQty);
  204: 
  205:       setQty(cleanQty);
  206:       setQtyInput(String(cleanQty));
  207: 
  208:       const nextUnitPrice = product ? getUnitPrice(cleanQty, product) : 0;
  209:       const nextTierQty =
  210:         cleanQty >= 100
  211:           ? 100
  212:           : cleanQty >= 50
  213:             ? 50
  214:             : cleanQty >= 12
  215:               ? 12
  216:               : cleanQty >= 3
  217:                 ? 3
  218:                 : 1;
  219: 
  220:       if (nextTierQty > lastTier) {
  221:         setShowUnlock(true);
  222:         window.setTimeout(() => setShowUnlock(false), 1400);
  223:       }
  224: 
  225:       if (nextUnitPrice !== unitPrice) {
  226:         setPricePulse(true);
  227:         window.setTimeout(() => setPricePulse(false), 220);
  228:       }
  229: 
  230:       setLastTier(nextTierQty);
  231:     },
  232:     [product, lastTier, unitPrice],
  233:   );
  234: 
  235:   const handleQtyInputChange = useCallback((value: string) => {
  236:     if (value === "" || /^\d+$/.test(value)) {
  237:       setQtyInput(value);
  238:     }
  239:   }, []);
  240: 
  241:   const handleQtyInputBlur = useCallback(() => {
  242:     if (!isQtyInputValid) {
  243:       setQtyInput(String(qty));
  244:       return;
  245:     }
  246: 
  247:     updateQty(effectiveQty);
  248:   }, [effectiveQty, isQtyInputValid, qty, updateQty]);
  249: 
  250:   const handleQtyInputKeyDown = useCallback(
  251:     (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
  252:       if (event.key === "Enter") {
  253:         event.currentTarget.blur();
  254:       }
  255:     },
  256:     [],
  257:   );
  258: 
  259:   const handleAddToCart = useCallback(() => {
  260:     if (!product || !available || !isQtyInputValid) return;
  261: 
  262:     addToCart(product, effectiveQty);
  263:     setCartOpen(true);
  264:   }, [product, available, isQtyInputValid, addToCart, effectiveQty]);
  265: 
  266:   const handleRelatedAddToCart = useCallback(
  267:     (relatedProduct: Product) => {
  268:       addToCart(relatedProduct, 1);
  269:       setSelectedRelated(relatedProduct);
  270:       setAddModalOpen(true);
  271:     },
  272:     [addToCart],
  273:   );
  274: 
  275:   const handleRelatedExtra = useCallback(
  276:     (qty: number) => {
  277:       if (!selectedRelated || qty <= 0) return;
  278:       addToCart(selectedRelated, qty);
  279:     },
  280:     [selectedRelated, addToCart],
  281:   );
  282: 
  283:   const handleRelatedOpenCart = useCallback(() => {
  284:     setAddModalOpen(false);
  285:     setCartOpen(true);
  286:   }, []);
  287: 
  288:   const handleWhatsApp = useCallback(() => {
  289:     if (!product) return;
  290: 
  291:     const message = [
  292:       "Hola Wooly, quiero consultar por este producto:",
  293:       "",
  294:       `Producto: ${product.title}`,
  295:       `Código: ${product.id}`,
  296:       `Cantidad: ${effectiveQty}`,
  297:       currentCategory ? `Categoría: ${currentCategory}` : "",
  298:       "",
  299:       `Link: ${window.location.href}`,
  300:     ]
  301:       .filter(Boolean)
  302:       .join("\n");
  303: 
  304:     window.open(
  305:       `https://wa.me/51936188636?text=${encodeURIComponent(message)}`,
  306:       "_blank",
  307:     );
  308:   }, [product, effectiveQty, currentCategory]);
  309: 
  310:   if (loading || !pageReady) return <ProductSkeleton />;
  311: 
  312:   if (!product) {
  313:     return (
  314:       <div className="min-h-screen bg-background flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4">
  315:         <p className="text-muted-foreground font-black text-lg">
  316:           Producto no encontrado
  317:         </p>
  318: 
  319:         <button
  320:           onClick={() => {
  321:             if (window.history.length > 1) {
  322:               navigate(-1);
  323:               return;
  324:             }
  325: 
  326:             navigate(
  327:               currentCategory
  328:                 ? `/catalogo/categoria.html?cat=${encodeURIComponent(currentCategory)}`
  329:                 : "/catalogo",
  330:             );
  331:           }}
  332:           className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-muted text-muted-foreground shadow-sm transition-all hover:bg-accent hover:text-foreground active:scale-95"
  333:           aria-label="Volver"
  334:         >
  335:           <ArrowLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
  336:         </button>
  337:       </div>
  338:     );
  339:   }
  340: 
  341:   return (
  342:     <div className="min-h-screen bg-background pb-28 md:pb-40">
  343:       <ProductSeo seo={productSeo} product={product} />
  344: 
  345:       <ProductDetailHeader
  346:         product={product}
  347:         onBack={handleBack}
  348:         onShare={handleShare}
  349:       />
  350: 
  351:       <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 mt-4 md:mt-10">
  352:         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,560px)_minmax(420px,1fr)] xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,620px)_minmax(460px,1fr)] lg:gap-8 xl:gap-10 items-start">
  353:           <ProductGallery
  354:             product={product}
  355:             available={available}
  356:             onZoom={(index) => setZoomGallery({ initialIndex: index })}
  357:           />
  358: 
  359:           <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:gap-6 card-shop p-4 md:p-7 bg-white">
  360:             <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2 md:justify-start">
  361:               <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-black text-slate-600">
  362:                 Código: {product.id}
  363:               </span>
  364: 
  365:               <span className="rounded-full bg-[#e6f6f8] px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-black capitalize text-[#1d8299]">
  366:                 {product.category}
  367:               </span>
  368:             </div>
  369: 
  370:             <div className="text-center md:text-left">

FILE: src\modules\cart\components\AddToCartModal.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { useEffect,useState } from "react";
    2: import { Package } from "lucide-react";
    3: import { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    4: import { AddToCartModalHeader } from "@/modules/cart/components/AddToCartModalHeader";
    5: import { AddToCartModalInfo } from "@/modules/cart/components/AddToCartModalInfo";
    6: 
    7: interface AddToCartModalProps{
    8:   open:boolean; product:Product|null; currentQty:number;
    9:   onClose:()=>void; onAddExtra:(qty:number)=>void; onOpenCart:()=>void;
   10:   secondaryActionLabel?:string; onSecondaryAction?:()=>void;
   11: }
   12: 
   13: function getNextTier(product:Product,qty:number){
   14:   return [
   15:     {targetQty:3,unitPrice:product.price_3},
   16:     {targetQty:12,unitPrice:product.price_12},
   17:     {targetQty:50,unitPrice:product.price_50},
   18:     {targetQty:100,unitPrice:product.price_100},
   19:   ].filter(t=>typeof t.unitPrice==="number"&&Number.isFinite(t.unitPrice)&&t.unitPrice>0)
   20:    .find(t=>qty<t.targetQty)??null;
   21: }
   22: 
   23: export function AddToCartModal({open,product,currentQty,onClose,onAddExtra,onOpenCart,secondaryActionLabel,onSecondaryAction}:AddToCartModalProps){
   24:   const [pulse,setPulse]=useState(false);
   25:   const nextTier=product?getNextTier(product,currentQty):null;
   26:   const missingQty=nextTier?Math.max(nextTier.targetQty-currentQty,0):0;
   27:   const hasUpsell=!!nextTier&&missingQty>0;
   28: 
   29:   useEffect(()=>{
   30:     if(!open||!nextTier)return;
   31:     setPulse(true);
   32:     const t=setTimeout(()=>setPulse(false),180);
   33:     return()=>clearTimeout(t);
   34:   },[currentQty,open,nextTier]);
   35: 
   36:   if(!open||!product)return null;
   37: 
   38:   return(
   39:     <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[130] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/45 px-4 backdrop-blur-[2px]">
   40:       <div className="w-full max-w-[360px] md:max-w-[500px] animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-200 rounded-2xl border border-[#dbe5ee] bg-white p-4 md:p-6 shadow-2xl">
   41: 
   42:         <AddToCartModalHeader product={product} onClose={onClose}/>
   43:         <AddToCartModalInfo product={product} currentQty={currentQty} pulse={pulse} nextTier={nextTier}/>
   44:         
   45:         <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
   46:           <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
   47:             {hasUpsell?(
   48:               <button onClick={()=>onAddExtra(Math.max((nextTier.targetQty??nextTier.qty)-currentQty,0))} className="w-full rounded-xl bg-[#1d8299] py-3 text-[13px] font-extrabold text-white transition-all hover:bg-[#16697a] active:scale-[.97]">
   49:                 (+{missingQty}) Mejorar precio
   50:               </button>
   51:             ):(
   52:               <button disabled className="w-full rounded-xl bg-[#dcfce7] py-3 text-[13px] font-bold text-[#16a34a]">
   53:                 ✓ Mejor precio
   54:               </button>
   55:             )}
   56: 
   57:             <button onClick={onSecondaryAction??onClose} className="w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-[#f6bfdc] bg-white px-3 py-3 text-[13px] font-extrabold text-[#f286be] transition-all hover:bg-[#fff0f7] active:scale-[.98]">
   58:               {secondaryActionLabel??"Otro producto"}
   59:             </button>
   60:           </div>
   61: 
   62:           <>
   63:           <button onClick={onOpenCart} className="hidden md:flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#1d8299]/30 bg-white py-3 text-[12px] font-bold text-[#1d8299] transition-all hover:bg-[#f0fafc] hover:border-[#1d8299] active:scale-[.98]">
   64:             <Package className="h-4 w-4"/>
   65:             Ver mi caja
   66:             <span className="rounded-full bg-[#1d8299] px-1.5 py-[2px] text-[10px] font-black text-white">
   67:               +{currentQty}
   68:             </span>
   69:           </button>
   70:           </>
   71:         </div>
   72: 
   73:       </div>
   74:     </div>
   75:   );
   76: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\components\AddToCartModalInfo.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: interface Props{
    4:   product:Product;
    5:   currentQty:number;
    6:   pulse:boolean;
    7:   nextTier:any;
    8: }
    9: 
   10: export function AddToCartModalInfo({product,currentQty,pulse,nextTier}:Props){
   11:   const tiers=[
   12:   {qty:1,price:product.price_1},
   13:   {qty:3,price:product.price_3},
   14:   {qty:12,price:product.price_12},
   15:   {qty:50,price:product.price_50},
   16:   {qty:100,price:product.price_100},
   17:   ].filter(t=>typeof t.price==="number"&&Number.isFinite(t.price)&&t.price>0)
   18: 
   19:   const currentTierClass=
   20:   currentQty>=100?"active-100":
   21:   currentQty>=50?"active-50":
   22:   currentQty>=12?"active-12":
   23:   currentQty>=3?"active-3":
   24:   "active-1";
   25: 
   26:   const bestTarget=tiers.at(-1)?.qty??1;
   27:   const targetQty=nextTier?.targetQty??nextTier?.qty??bestTarget;
   28:   const unitPrice=nextTier?.unitPrice??nextTier?.price??product.price_1;
   29:   const missingQty=Math.max(targetQty-currentQty,0);
   30:   const activeTiers=tiers.map(t=>t.qty);
   31:   const currentTierIndex=activeTiers.reduce((acc,qty,i)=>currentQty>=qty?i:acc,0);
   32:   const nextTierIndex=activeTiers.findIndex(qty=>currentQty<qty);
   33:   const nextIndex=nextTierIndex===-1?activeTiers.length-1:nextTierIndex;
   34:   const prevQty=activeTiers[currentTierIndex]??1;
   35:   const nextQty=activeTiers[nextIndex]??prevQty;
   36:   const segmentBase=activeTiers.length>1?100/(activeTiers.length-1):100;
   37:   const segmentProgress=nextQty>prevQty?((currentQty-prevQty)/(nextQty-prevQty))*segmentBase:0;
   38:   const rawProgress=Math.min((currentTierIndex*segmentBase)+segmentProgress,100);
   39:   const progress=currentQty>0?Math.max(rawProgress,10):0;
   40:   const unlocked=currentQty>=bestTarget;
   41: 
   42:   return(
   43:     <div className={["mt-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-[#f8fafc] p-2.5 transition-transform",pulse?"scale-[1.02]":""].join(" ")}>
   44:       <div className="relative mb-2">
   45:         <img src={product.img||"/placeholder.svg"} alt={product.title} className="h-[320px] md:h-[420px] w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white object-cover"/>
   46: 
   47:         <div className={`absolute left-1/2 bottom-3 grid h-12 min-w-[50px] -translate-x-1/2 place-items-center rounded-full px-3 text-[13px] font-black text-white shadow-lg animate-pulse ${currentTierClass}`}>
   48:           +{currentQty}
   49:         </div>
   50:     </div>
   51:       <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
   52:         <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-[3px] text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-500">Código: {product.id}</span>
   53:         <span className="rounded-full bg-[#e6f6f8] px-2 py-[3px] text-[10px] font-black capitalize text-[#1d8299]">{product.category}</span>
   54:       </div>
   55: 
   56:       <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-white p-3 shadow-sm">
   57:         <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-[12px] md:text-[14px] font-black">
   58:           <span className="text-slate-600">Ya tienes {currentQty} productos</span>
   59:           <span className={unlocked?"text-emerald-600":"text-orange-500"}>{currentQty}/{bestTarget}</span>
   60:         </div>
   61: 
   62:         <div className="h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200">
   63:           <div className={["h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500",unlocked?"bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-green-600":"bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 via-amber-400 to-emerald-500"].join(" ")} style={{width:`${progress}%`}}/>
   64:         </div>
   65: 
   66:         {tiers.length>0&&(
   67:           <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
   68:             {tiers.map(t=>{
   69:               const active=currentQty>=t.qty;
   70:               const cls=`active-${t.qty}`;
   71: 
   72:               return(
   73:                 <button
   74:                   key={t.qty}
   75:                   disabled
   76:                   className={[
   77:                     "rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] md:text-[13px] font-black transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2",
   78:                     active?cls:"cart-tier-btn-muted"
   79:                   ].join(" ")}
   80:                 >
   81:                   <span>{t.qty}+</span>
   82:                   <span>S/{t.price!.toFixed(1)}</span>
   83:                 </button>
   84:               );
   85:             })}
   86:           </div>
   87:         )}
   88: 
   89:         <p className="mt-2 text-center text-[13px] md:text-[16px] font-bold leading-snug text-slate-700">
   90:           {unlocked?(<>🎉 Mejor precio desbloqueado</>):nextTier?(<>🚀 Agrega <span className="text-[#1d8299]">{missingQty}</span> más y paga <span className="text-[#1d8299]">S/{unitPrice.toFixed(1)}</span> c/u</>):(<>✅ Ya tienes el mejor precio disponible</>)}
   91:         </p>
   92:       </div>
   93:     </div>
   94:   );
   95: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\components\CartQtyInput.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    2: import { CartItem } from "@/shared/types/product";
    3: 
    4: interface CartQtyInputProps {
    5:   item: CartItem;
    6:   onSetQty: (id: string, qty: number | null) => void;
    7: }
    8: 
    9: export function CartQtyInput({
   10:   item,
   11:   onSetQty,
   12: }: CartQtyInputProps) {
   13:   const [qtyInput, setQtyInput] = useState(String(item.qty));
   14:   const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
   15: 
   16:   useEffect(() => {
   17:     if (!isEditing) {
   18:       setQtyInput(String(item.qty));
   19:     }
   20:   }, [item.qty, isEditing]);
   21: 
   22:   return (
   23:     <input
   24:       type="text"
   25:       inputMode="numeric"
   26:       pattern="[0-9]*"
   27:       value={qtyInput}
   28:       onFocus={() => setIsEditing(true)}
   29:       onBlur={() => setIsEditing(false)}
   30:       onChange={(event) => {
   31:         const value = event.target.value;
   32: 
   33:         if (value === "") {
   34:           setQtyInput("");
   35:           onSetQty(item.id, null);
   36:           return;
   37:         }
   38: 
   39:         if (!/^\d+$/.test(value)) return;
   40: 
   41:         setQtyInput(value);
   42:         onSetQty(item.id, parseInt(value, 10));
   43:       }}
   44:       className="w-12 text-center text-[14px] font-black tracking-tight text-[#0f172a] bg-transparent outline-none [appearance:textfield] [&::-webkit-outer-spin-button]:appearance-none [&::-webkit-inner-spin-button]:appearance-none"
   45:       aria-label={`Cantidad de ${item.title}`}
   46:     />
   47:   );
   48: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\components\CartRow.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
    2: import { Trash2, Zap } from "lucide-react";
    3: 
    4: import { CartItem } from "@/shared/types/product";
    5: import { getEffectivePrice } from "@/modules/catalog/utils/products";
    6: import { getActiveTierQty } from "@/modules/cart/utils/getActiveTierQty";
    7: import { getTierUnlockMessage } from "@/modules/cart/utils/getTierUnlockMessage";
    8: 
    9: import { CartTierSelector } from "@/modules/cart/components/CartTierSelector";
   10: import { CartQtyControls } from "@/modules/cart/components/CartQtyControls";
   11: import { CartNoteTextarea } from "@/modules/cart/components/CartNoteTextarea";
   12: 
   13: interface CartRowProps {
   14:   item: CartItem;
   15:   onRemove: (id: string) => void;
   16:   onChangeQty: (id: string, delta: number) => void;
   17:   onSetQty: (id: string, qty: number | null) => void;
   18:   onChangeNote: (id: string, note: string) => void;
   19: }
   20: 
   21: export function CartRow({
   22:   item,
   23:   onRemove,
   24:   onChangeQty,
   25:   onSetQty,
   26:   onChangeNote,
   27: }: CartRowProps) {
   28:   const activePrice = getEffectivePrice(item);
   29:   const subtotal = activePrice * item.qty;
   30:   const activeTierQty = getActiveTierQty(item);
   31:   
   32:   const prevQtyRef = useRef(item.qty);
   33:   const prevPriceRef = useRef(activePrice);
   34:   const prevTierRef = useRef(activeTierQty);
   35: 
   36:   const [qtyPulse, setQtyPulse] = useState(false);
   37:   const [pricePulse, setPricePulse] = useState(false);
   38:   const [tierFlash, setTierFlash] = useState(false);
   39: 
   40:   useEffect(() => {
   41:     if (prevQtyRef.current !== item.qty) {
   42:       setQtyPulse(true);
   43: 
   44:       const timer = setTimeout(() => setQtyPulse(false), 220);
   45: 
   46:       prevQtyRef.current = item.qty;
   47: 
   48:       return () => clearTimeout(timer);
   49:     }
   50:   }, [item.qty]);
   51: 
   52:   useEffect(() => {
   53:     if (prevPriceRef.current !== activePrice) {
   54:       setPricePulse(true);
   55: 
   56:       const timer = setTimeout(() => setPricePulse(false), 280);
   57: 
   58:       prevPriceRef.current = activePrice;
   59: 
   60:       return () => clearTimeout(timer);
   61:     }
   62:   }, [activePrice]);
   63: 
   64:   useEffect(() => {
   65:     if (prevTierRef.current !== activeTierQty) {
   66:       setTierFlash(true);
   67: 
   68:       const timer = setTimeout(() => setTierFlash(false), 1500);
   69: 
   70:       prevTierRef.current = activeTierQty;
   71: 
   72:       return () => clearTimeout(timer);
   73:     }
   74:   }, [activeTierQty]);
   75: 
   76:   return (
   77:     <div className={`cart-item-card ${qtyPulse ? "scale-[1.01]" : ""}`}>
   78:       <div className="flex gap-4">
   79:         <div className="cart-product-img">
   80:           <img
   81:             src={item.img}
   82:             alt={item.title}
   83:             className="w-full h-full object-cover"
   84:           />
   85:         </div>
   86: 
   87:         <div className="flex-grow text-left min-w-0">
   88:           <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-3">
   89:             <div className="min-w-0">
   90:               <h4 className="text-[13px] font-extrabold text-[#0f172a] leading-tight tracking-tight capitalize">
   91:                 {item.title}
   92:               </h4>
   93: 
   94:               <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-[#94a3b8] mt-1 uppercase tracking-wide">
   95:                 {item.id}
   96:               </p>
   97:             </div>
   98: 
   99:             <button
  100:               onClick={() => onRemove(item.id)}
  101:               className="text-[#cbd5e1] hover:text-[#ef4444] transition-colors flex-shrink-0"
  102:             >
  103:               <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
  104:             </button>
  105:           </div>
  106: 
  107:           <div className="mt-2 flex items-end justify-between gap-3">
  108:             <div className={`text-[12px] font-black tracking-tight ${pricePulse?"text-[#1d8299]":"text-[#64748b]"}`}>
  109:               {item.qty}u  × S/ {activePrice.toFixed(2)} c/u
  110:             </div>
  111: 
  112:             <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1">
  113:               <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-[#94a3b8]">S/</span>
  114:               <span className={`text-2xl font-black tracking-tighter transition-all duration-300 ${pricePulse?"scale-105 text-[#1d8299]":"text-[#0f172a]"}`}>
  115:                 {subtotal.toFixed(2)}
  116:               </span>
  117:             </div>
  118:           </div>
  119:         </div>
  120:       </div>
  121: 
  122:       <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
  123:         <CartTierSelector item={item} onSetQty={onSetQty} />
  124: 
  125:         <CartQtyControls
  126:           item={item}
  127:           qtyPulse={qtyPulse}
  128:           onChangeQty={onChangeQty}
  129:           onSetQty={onSetQty}
  130:         />
  131:       </div>
  132: 
  133:       <CartNoteTextarea item={item} onChangeNote={onChangeNote} />
  134:     </div>
  135:   );
  136: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\components\CartTierSelector.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { CartItem } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import { CART_TIERS,TIER_COLORS } from "@/shared/config/cartTiers";
    3: 
    4: interface CartTierSelectorProps{
    5:   item:CartItem;
    6:   onSetQty:(id:string,qty:number|null)=>void;
    7: }
    8: 
    9: export function CartTierSelector({item,onSetQty}:CartTierSelectorProps){
   10:   const itemTiers=CART_TIERS.filter(tier=>{
   11:     const value=item[tier.key];
   12:     return value!==null&&value!==undefined&&value>0;
   13:   });
   14: 
   15:   const gridCols=
   16:     itemTiers.length<=1?"grid-cols-1":
   17:     itemTiers.length===2?"grid-cols-2":
   18:     "grid-cols-3";
   19: 
   20:   return(
   21:     <div className={`grid ${gridCols} gap-1 flex-1`}>
   22:       {itemTiers.map((tier,index)=>{
   23:         const nextTier=itemTiers[index+1];
   24:         const isActive=item.qty>=tier.qty&&(!nextTier||item.qty<nextTier.qty);
   25: 
   26:         return(
   27:           <button
   28:             key={tier.qty}
   29:             type="button"
   30:             onClick={()=>onSetQty(item.id,tier.qty)}
   31:             className={[
   32:               "cart-tier-btn",
   33:               "w-full py-1.5",
   34:               "transition-all duration-300",
   35:               isActive?`${TIER_COLORS[tier.cls]} scale-[1.02] shadow-md`:"cart-tier-btn-muted hover:scale-[1.02]"
   36:             ].join(" ")}
   37:           >
   38:             {tier.label}
   39:           </button>
   40:         );
   41:       })}
   42:     </div>
   43:   );
   44: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\store\cart.actions.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { CartItem, Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: export function addItemToCart(
    4:   cart: CartItem[],
    5:   product: Product,
    6:   qty: number
    7: ): CartItem[] {
    8:   const safeQty = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(qty) || 1));
    9: 
   10:   const existing = cart.find((x) => x.id === product.id);
   11: 
   12:   if (existing) {
   13:     return cart.map((x) =>
   14:       x.id === product.id
   15:         ? {
   16:             ...x,
   17:             qty: x.qty + safeQty,
   18:           }
   19:         : x
   20:     );
   21:   }
   22: 
   23:   return [
   24:     ...cart,
   25:     {
   26:       ...product,
   27:       qty: safeQty,
   28:       note: "",
   29:     },
   30:   ];
   31: }
   32: 
   33: export function removeItemFromCart(
   34:   cart: CartItem[],
   35:   id: string
   36: ): CartItem[] {
   37:   return cart.filter((x) => x.id !== id);
   38: }
   39: 
   40: export function changeCartItemQty(
   41:   cart: CartItem[],
   42:   id: string,
   43:   delta: number
   44: ): CartItem[] {
   45:   return cart
   46:     .map((x) => {
   47:       if (x.id !== id) return x;
   48: 
   49:       const newQty = x.qty + delta;
   50: 
   51:       if (newQty <= 0) return null;
   52: 
   53:       return {
   54:         ...x,
   55:         qty: newQty,
   56:       };
   57:     })
   58:     .filter(Boolean) as CartItem[];
   59: }
   60: 
   61: export function setCartItemQty(
   62:   cart: CartItem[],
   63:   id: string,
   64:   qty: number | null
   65: ): CartItem[] {
   66:   return cart
   67:     .map((x) => {
   68:       if (x.id !== id) return x;
   69: 
   70:       if (qty === null) return x;
   71: 
   72:       const safeQty = Math.floor(Number(qty) || 0);
   73: 
   74:       if (safeQty <= 0) return null;
   75: 
   76:       return {
   77:         ...x,
   78:         qty: safeQty,
   79:       };
   80:     })
   81:     .filter(Boolean) as CartItem[];
   82: }
   83: 
   84: export function setCartItemNote(
   85:   cart: CartItem[],
   86:   id: string,
   87:   note: string
   88: ): CartItem[] {
   89:   return cart.map((x) =>
   90:     x.id === id
   91:       ? {
   92:           ...x,
   93:           note: note ?? "",
   94:         }
   95:       : x
   96:   );
   97: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\store\cart.context.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
    2: import { useCart } from "./cart.store";
    3: 
    4: type CartStore = ReturnType<typeof useCart>;
    5: 
    6: const CartContext = createContext<CartStore | null>(null);
    7: 
    8: export function CartProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    9:   const cartStore = useCart();
   10: 
   11:   return (
   12:     <CartContext.Provider value={cartStore}>
   13:       {children}
   14:     </CartContext.Provider>
   15:   );
   16: }
   17: 
   18: export function useCartStore() {
   19:   const context = useContext(CartContext);
   20: 
   21:   if (!context) {
   22:     throw new Error("useCartStore debe usarse dentro de CartProvider");
   23:   }
   24: 
   25:   return context;
   26: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\store\cart.persistence.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { CartItem } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: export const CART_KEY = "wooly_cart";
    4: 
    5: export function loadCart(): CartItem[] {
    6:   try {
    7:     const saved = localStorage.getItem(CART_KEY);
    8:     return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : [];
    9:   } catch {
   10:     return [];
   11:   }
   12: }
   13: 
   14: export function saveCart(cart: CartItem[]) {
   15:   try {
   16:     localStorage.setItem(CART_KEY, JSON.stringify(cart));
   17:   } catch {
   18:     // noop
   19:   }
   20: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\store\cart.selectors.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { CartItem } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import { getEffectivePrice } from "@/modules/catalog/utils/products";
    3: 
    4: function toMoney(value: unknown) {
    5:   const numberValue = Number(value);
    6:   return Number.isFinite(numberValue) ? numberValue : 0;
    7: }
    8: 
    9: export function getTotalItems(cart: CartItem[]) {
   10:   return cart.reduce(
   11:     (acc, item) => acc + toMoney(item.qty),
   12:     0
   13:   );
   14: }
   15: 
   16: export function getTotalPrice(cart: CartItem[]) {
   17:   return cart.reduce((acc, item) => {
   18:     const qty = toMoney(item.qty);
   19:     const price = toMoney(getEffectivePrice(item, qty));
   20: 
   21:     return acc + price * qty;
   22:   }, 0);
   23: }
   24: 
   25: export function getTotalOriginal(cart: CartItem[]) {
   26:   return cart.reduce((acc, item) => {
   27:     const qty = toMoney(item.qty);
   28:     const basePrice = toMoney(item.price_1);
   29: 
   30:     return acc + basePrice * qty;
   31:   }, 0);
   32: }
   33: 
   34: export function getTotalSavings(cart: CartItem[]) {
   35:   return Math.max(
   36:     0,
   37:     getTotalOriginal(cart) - getTotalPrice(cart)
   38:   );
   39: }

============================================================
FILE: src\modules\cart\store\cart.store.ts
============================================================

    1: import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
    2: import { CartItem, Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    3: 
    4: import {
    5:   getTotalItems,
    6:   getTotalPrice,
    7:   getTotalSavings,
    8: } from "./cart.selectors";
    9: 
   10: import {
   11:   addItemToCart,
   12:   removeItemFromCart,
   13:   changeCartItemQty,
   14:   setCartItemQty,
   15:   setCartItemNote,
   16: } from "./cart.actions";
   17: 
   18: import {
   19:   CART_KEY,
   20:   loadCart,
   21:   saveCart,
   22: } from "./cart.persistence";
   23: 
   24: export function useCart() {
   25:   const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartItem[]>(() => loadCart());
   26: 
   27:   useEffect(() => {
   28:     saveCart(cart);
   29:   }, [cart]);
   30: 
   31:   useEffect(() => {
   32:     const handler = (e: StorageEvent) => {
   33:       if (e.key !== CART_KEY) return;
   34: 
   35:       try {
   36:         setCart(e.newValue ? JSON.parse(e.newValue) : []);
   37:       } catch {
   38:         setCart([]);
   39:       }
   40:     };
   41: 
   42:     window.addEventListener("storage", handler);
   43: 
   44:     return () => window.removeEventListener("storage", handler);
   45:   }, []);
   46: 
   47:   const addToCart = useCallback(
   48:     (product: Product, qty: number = 1) => {
   49:       setCart((prev) =>
   50:         addItemToCart(prev, product, qty)
   51:       );
   52:     },
   53:     []
   54:   );
   55: 
   56:   const removeFromCart = useCallback((id: string) => {
   57:     setCart((prev) =>
   58:       removeItemFromCart(prev, id)
   59:     );
   60:   }, []);
   61: 
   62:   const changeQty = useCallback(
   63:     (id: string, delta: number) => {
   64:       setCart((prev) =>
   65:         changeCartItemQty(prev, id, delta)
   66:       );
   67:     },
   68:     []
   69:   );
   70: 
   71:   const setExactQty = useCallback(
   72:     (id: string, qty: number | null) => {
   73:       setCart((prev) =>
   74:         setCartItemQty(prev, id, qty)
   75:       );
   76:     },
   77:     []
   78:   );
   79: 
   80:   const setItemNote = useCallback(
   81:     (id: string, note: string) => {
   82:       setCart((prev) =>
   83:         setCartItemNote(prev, id, note)
   84:       );
   85:     },
   86:     []
   87:   );
   88: 
   89:   const clearCart = useCallback(() => {
   90:     setCart([]);
   91:   }, []);
   92: 
   93:   const totalItems = getTotalItems(cart);
   94: 
   95:   const totalPrice = getTotalPrice(cart);
   96: 
   97:   const savings = getTotalSavings(cart);
   98: 
   99:   return {
  100:     cart,
  101:     addToCart,
  102:     removeFromCart,
  103:     changeQty,
  104:     setExactQty,
  105:     setItemNote,
  106:     clearCart,
  107:     totalItems,
  108:     totalPrice,
  109:     savings,
  110:   };
  111: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\utils\checkout.ts
============================================================

    1: import { CartItem } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import { getEffectivePrice } from "@/modules/catalog/utils/products";
    3: 
    4: export function checkout(
    5:   cart: CartItem[],
    6:   total: string,
    7:   savings: number,
    8:   onClearCart: () => void,
    9:   onClose: () => void
   10: ) {
   11:   if (cart.length === 0) return;
   12: 
   13:   let message = "*NUEVO PEDIDO WOOLY - MAYORISTAS*\n\n";
   14:   message += "Hola, deseo pedir lo siguiente:\n\n";
   15: 
   16:   cart.forEach((item) => {
   17:     const price = getEffectivePrice(item);
   18:     const subtotal = price * item.qty;
   19:     const note = item.note?.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   20: 
   21:     message += `• *[ ${item.id} ]* | *${item.title}*\n`;
   22:     message += `  Cantidad: ${item.qty} u\n`;
   23:     message += `  Precio: S/${price.toFixed(2)}\n`;
   24:     message += `  Subtotal: S/${subtotal.toFixed(2)}\n`;
   25: 
   26:     if (note) {
   27:       message += `  Detalle: ${note}\n`;
   28:     }
   29: 
   30:     message += "\n";
   31:   });
   32: 
   33:   message += "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n";
   34:   message += `*Total estimado: S/${total}*\n`;
   35: 
   36:   if (savings > 0) {
   37:     message += `Ahorro estimado: S/${savings.toFixed(2)}\n`;
   38:   }
   39: 
   40:   message += "\nConfirmar disponibilidad, gracias.";
   41: 
   42:   const url = `https://wa.me/51936188636?text=${encodeURIComponent(message)}`;
   43:   window.open(url, "_blank");
   44: 
   45:   setTimeout(() => {
   46:     onClearCart();
   47:     onClose();
   48:   }, 300);
   49: }

FILE: src\modules\cart\utils\getActiveTierQty.ts
============================================================

    1: import { CartItem } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: export function getActiveTierQty(item: CartItem): number {
    4:   if (item.price_100 && item.qty >= 100) return 100;
    5:   if (item.price_50 && item.qty >= 50) return 50;
    6:   if (item.price_12 && item.qty >= 12) return 12;
    7:   if (item.price_3 && item.qty >= 3) return 3;
    8: 
    9:   return 1;
   10: }

    1: import { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: export interface NextTier {
    4: 
    5: targetQty:number;
    6: 
    7: unitPrice:number;
    8: 
    9: }
   10: 
   11: export function getNextTier(
   12: 
   13: product:Product,
   14: qty:number
   15: 
   16: ):NextTier|null{
   17: 
   18: const tiers=[
   19: 
   20: {
   21: 
   22: targetQty:3,
   23: unitPrice:product.price_3
   24: 
   25: },
   26: 
   27: {
   28: 
   29: targetQty:12,
   30: unitPrice:product.price_12
   31: 
   32: },
   33: 
   34: {
   35: 
   36: targetQty:50,
   37: unitPrice:product.price_50
   38: 
   39: },
   40: 
   41: {
   42: 
   43: targetQty:100,
   44: unitPrice:product.price_100
   45: 
   46: }
   47: 
   48: ];
   49: 
   50: const valid=
   51: 
   52: tiers.filter(
   53: 
   54: tier=>
   55: 
   56: typeof tier.unitPrice==="number"
   57: 
   58: &&
   59: 
   60: Number.isFinite(
   61: tier.unitPrice
   62: )
   63: 
   64: &&
   65: 
   66: tier.unitPrice>0
   67: 
   68: );
   69: 
   70: return(
   71: 
   72: valid.find(
   73: 
   74: tier=>
   75: 
   76: qty<tier.targetQty
   77: 
   78: )
   79: 
   80: ??null
   81: 
   82: );
   83: 
   84: }

    1: import { CartItem } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import { getActiveTierQty } from "@/modules/cart/utils/getActiveTierQty";
    3: 
    4: export function getTierUnlockMessage(item: CartItem): string | null {
    5:   const activeTier = getActiveTierQty(item);
    6: 
    7:   if (activeTier >= 100) return "🔥 Precio por cajón activado";

   13: }

    1: import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

   11:

   13:   product: Product;
   14:   cart?: CartItem[];

   17: }
   18: 
   19: export function ProductCard({
   20:   product: p,
   21:   cart = [],
   22:   onAddToCart,
   23:   onImageClick,
   24: }: Props) {
   25:   const navigate = useNavigate();
   26:

   28:     available,

   37: 
   38:   const goToDetail = () =>
   39:     navigate(`/catalogo/producto.html?id=${p.id}&cat=${p.category}`);
   40: 
   41:   const handleCardClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
   42:     const selectedText = window.getSelection()?.toString().trim();
   43:     if (selectedText) return;
   44:

   46:     if (target.closest("[data-no-card-click],button,a,input,textarea,select"))
   47:       return;
   48:

   50:   };
   51:

   53:     ? "Consultar reposición"
   54:     : showWhatsAppButton
   55:       ? "Consultar"

   57:         ? `Sumar (${qtyInCart})`

   59:

   62:       onClick={handleCardClick}

   65:       <div
   66:         onClick={(e) => {
   67:           e.stopPropagation();
   68:           onImageClick?.(p);
   69:         }}

   71:       >
   72:         <ProductCardBadges product={p} />
   73: 
   74:         {isInCart && (
   75:           <div className="absolute right-2 top-2 z-20 rounded-full bg-[#1d8299] px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-black leading-none text-white shadow-[0_6px_16px_rgba(29,130,153,.25)]">
   76:             +{qtyInCart}
   77:           </div>
   78:         )}
   79:

   83:               Agotado
   84:             </span>
   85:           </div>
   86:         )}
   87: 
   88:         <img
   89:           src={p.img || "/placeholder.svg"}
   90:           alt={p.title}

   93:             isAgotado ? "opacity-90 saturate-[.9]" : "",
   94:           ].join(" ")}
   95:           loading="lazy"

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: interface ProductCardPriceProps {
    4:   product: Product;
    5:   isPreventa?: boolean;
    6: }
    7: 
    8: export function ProductCardPrice({
    9:   product,
   10:   isPreventa = false,
   11: }: ProductCardPriceProps) {
   12:   const hasOffer =
   13:     !!product.price_offer &&
   14:     product.price_offer > 0 &&
   15:     product.price_offer < product.price_1;
   16:   const finalPrice = hasOffer ? product.price_offer! : product.price_1;
   17: 
   18:   if (isPreventa) {
   19:     return (
   20:       <div className="mt-2.5 flex flex-col items-center gap-1 border-t border-[#eef2f6] pt-2.5">

   28:           Más información por WhatsApp
   29:         </span>
   30:       </div>
   31:     );
   32:   }
   33:

   35:     <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col items-center gap-1 border-t border-[#eef2f6] pt-2">

   45:             hasOffer ? "text-red-600" : "text-[#1d8299]"

   48:           {finalPrice.toFixed(1)}
   49:         </span>
   50:

   52:           <span className="mb-[2px] text-[12px] font-bold text-slate-400 line-through decoration-2">

   54:           </span>

   56:       </div>
   57:     </div>

FILE: src\modules\catalog\components\ProductCardStock.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { CheckCircle,AlertTriangle,Clock,XCircle } from "lucide-react";
    2: 
    3: interface ProductCardStockProps{stock:number|null|undefined;price:number;status?:string;}
    4: 
    5: export function ProductCardStock({stock,price,status}:ProductCardStockProps){
    6:   const isPreventa=(status||"").trim().toLowerCase()==="preventa";
    7:   let stockText="",stockColorClass="",StockIcon:typeof CheckCircle=CheckCircle;
    8: 
    9:   if(isPreventa){stockText="Preventa";stockColorClass="bg-green-100 text-green-700";StockIcon=Clock;}
   10:   else if(!price||price<=0||stock==null){stockText="Próximo";stockColorClass="bg-muted text-muted-foreground";StockIcon=Clock;}
   11:   else if(stock===0){stockText="Agotado";stockColorClass="bg-destructive/10 text-destructive";StockIcon=XCircle;}
   12:   else if(stock<=12){stockText=`Últimas ${stock}`;stockColorClass="bg-red-100 text-red-600";StockIcon=AlertTriangle;}
   13:   else if(stock<=36){stockText="Stock limitado";stockColorClass="bg-orange-100 text-orange-600";StockIcon=AlertTriangle;}
   14:   else if(stock<=50){stockText="Disponible";stockColorClass="bg-green-100 text-green-700";StockIcon=CheckCircle;}
   15:   else{stockText="Alto stock";stockColorClass="bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700";StockIcon=CheckCircle;}
   16: 
   17:   return(
   18:     <span className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-[3px] text-[9px] font-black ${stockColorClass}`}>
   19:       <StockIcon className="h-3 w-3"/>
   20:       {stockText}
   21:     </span>
   22:   );
   23: }

FILE: src\modules\catalog\components\ProductCardTierBadges.tsx
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import { getAvailablePriceTiers } from "@/shared/config/priceTiers";
    3: 
    4: const CHIP_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
    5:   price_3: "wholesale-chip-price-3",
    6:   price_12: "wholesale-chip-price-12",
    7:   price_50: "wholesale-chip-price-50",
    8:   price_100: "wholesale-chip-price-100",
    9: };
   10: 
   11: const ICONS: Record<string, string> = {
   12:   price_3: "🔥",
   13:   price_12: "⚡",
   14:   price_50: "🚀",
   15:   price_100: "💎",
   16: };
   17: 
   18: const LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
   19:   price_3: "Por Mayor",
   20:   price_12: "Por Docena",
   21:   price_50: "Medio ciento",
   22:   price_100: "Por Caja",
   23: };
   24: 
   25: interface Props {
   26:   product: Product;
   27:   available: boolean;
   28:   isPreventa: boolean;
   29: }
   30: 
   31: export function ProductCardTierBadges({
   32:   product,
   33:   available,
   34:   isPreventa,
   35: }: Props) {
   36:   if (!available || isPreventa) return null;
   37: 
   38:   const tiers = getAvailablePriceTiers(product).filter((tier) => {
   39:     const price = Number(product[tier.key]);
   40:     return tier.key !== "price_1" && price > 0;
   41:   });
   42: 
   43:   if (!tiers.length) return null;
   44: 
   45:   return (
   46:     <div className="wholesale-list">
   47:       <div className="wholesale-title mt-1 pt-1.5 border-t border-dashed border-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600">
   48:         <span>📦</span>
   49:         <span>PRECIOS MAYORISTA</span>
   50:       </div>
   51: 
   52:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
   53:         {tiers.map((tier) => {
   54:           const price = Number(product[tier.key]);
   55:           if (price >= product.price_1) return null;
   56: 
   57:           const units = parseInt(tier.label, 10);
   58:           const total = price * units;
   59: 
   60:           return (
   61:             <div
   62:               key={tier.key}
   63:               className={`wholesale-chip flex justify-center text-center ${
   64:                 CHIP_COLORS[tier.key] ||
   65:                 "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600"
   66:               }`}
   67:             >
   68:               <span className="block w-full text-center">
   69:                 {LABELS[tier.key]} ({tier.label}) x S/{total.toFixed(0)}
   70:               </span>
   71:             </div>
   72:           );
   73:         })}
   74:       </div>
   75:     </div>
   76:   );
   77: }

FILE: src\modules\catalog\hooks\useCatalogData.ts
============================================================

    1: import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
    2: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    3: import type { CatalogCategoryId } from "@/modules/catalog/services/productsConfig";
    4: import {
    5:   getCachedCatalogSnapshot,
    6:   getCatalogCategories,
    7:   loadCategoryProducts,
    8:   type CatalogCategory,
    9: } from "@/modules/catalog/services/fetchProducts";
   10: 
   11: type CategoryLoadStatus = "idle" | "loading" | "loaded" | "error";
   12: 
   13: type ProductsByCategory = Partial<Record<CatalogCategoryId, Product[]>>;
   14: type StatusByCategory = Record<CatalogCategoryId, CategoryLoadStatus>;
   15: 
   16: const CATALOG_CATEGORIES = getCatalogCategories();
   17: 
   18: const sortProducts = (items: Product[]) =>
   19:   [...items].sort((a, b) => (b.priority ?? 0) - (a.priority ?? 0));
   20: 
   21: const flattenByCategory = (byCategory: ProductsByCategory) =>
   22:   sortProducts(Object.values(byCategory).flatMap((items) => items ?? []));
   23: 
   24: const createInitialStatus = (
   25:   loadedCategories: CatalogCategoryId[],
   26: ): StatusByCategory => {
   27:   const loaded = new Set<CatalogCategoryId>(loadedCategories);
   28: 
   29:   return CATALOG_CATEGORIES.reduce((acc, category) => {
   30:     acc[category] = loaded.has(category) ? "loaded" : "idle";
   31:     return acc;
   32:   }, {} as StatusByCategory);
   33: };
   34: 
   35: export function useCatalogData(activeCategory: CatalogCategory = "todas") {
   36:   const initialSnapshotRef = useRef(getCachedCatalogSnapshot());
   37: 
   38:   const [productsByCategory, setProductsByCategory] =
   39:     useState<ProductsByCategory>(() => initialSnapshotRef.current.byCategory);
   40: 
   41:   const [statusByCategory, setStatusByCategory] = useState<StatusByCategory>(
   42:     () => createInitialStatus(initialSnapshotRef.current.loadedCategories),
   43:   );
   44: 
   45:   const [isBootstrapping, setIsBootstrapping] = useState(
   46:     () => initialSnapshotRef.current.products.length === 0,
   47:   );
   48: 
   49:   const mountedRef = useRef(false);
   50:   const productsByCategoryRef = useRef(productsByCategory);
   51:   const statusByCategoryRef = useRef(statusByCategory);
   52: 
   53:   useEffect(() => {
   54:     mountedRef.current = true;
   55: 
   56:     return () => {
   57:       mountedRef.current = false;
   58:     };
   59:   }, []);
   60: 
   61:   const syncProductsByCategory = useCallback((next: ProductsByCategory) => {
   62:     productsByCategoryRef.current = next;
   63:     setProductsByCategory(next);
   64:   }, []);
   65: 
   66:   const syncStatusByCategory = useCallback((next: StatusByCategory) => {
   67:     statusByCategoryRef.current = next;
   68:     setStatusByCategory(next);
   69:   }, []);
   70: 
   71:   const setCategoryStatus = useCallback(
   72:     (category: CatalogCategoryId, status: CategoryLoadStatus) => {
   73:       const next = {
   74:         ...statusByCategoryRef.current,
   75:         [category]: status,
   76:       };
   77: 
   78:       syncStatusByCategory(next);
   79:     },
   80:     [syncStatusByCategory],
   81:   );
   82: 
   83:   const setCategoryProducts = useCallback(
   84:     (category: CatalogCategoryId, products: Product[]) => {
   85:       const next = {
   86:         ...productsByCategoryRef.current,
   87:         [category]: products,
   88:       };
   89: 
   90:       syncProductsByCategory(next);
   91:     },
   92:     [syncProductsByCategory],
   93:   );
   94: 
   95:   const ensureCategoryLoaded = useCallback(
   96:     async (category: CatalogCategoryId) => {
   97:       const currentStatus = statusByCategoryRef.current[category];
   98: 
   99:       if (currentStatus === "loaded" || currentStatus === "loading") {
  100:         return;
  101:       }
  102: 
  103:       setCategoryStatus(category, "loading");
  104: 
  105:       try {
  106:         const products = await loadCategoryProducts(category);
  107: 
  108:         if (!mountedRef.current) return;
  109: 
  110:         setCategoryProducts(category, products);
  111:         setCategoryStatus(category, "loaded");
  112:       } catch (error) {
  113:         console.error(`Error cargando categoría "${category}":`, error);
  114: 
  115:         if (!mountedRef.current) return;
  116: 
  117:         setCategoryProducts(category, []);
  118:         setCategoryStatus(category, "error");
  119:       } finally {
  120:         if (mountedRef.current) {
  121:           setIsBootstrapping(false);
  122:         }
  123:       }
  124:     },
  125:     [setCategoryProducts, setCategoryStatus],
  126:   );
  127: 
  128:   useEffect(() => {
  129:     let cancelled = false;
  130: 
  131:     const run = async () => {
  132:       const preferredCategory =
  133:         activeCategory !== "todas" ? activeCategory : CATALOG_CATEGORIES[0];
  134: 
  135:       if (preferredCategory) {
  136:         await ensureCategoryLoaded(preferredCategory);
  137:       }
  138: 
  139:       if (cancelled) return;
  140: 
  141:       /*
  142:         Precarga suave:
  143:         carga el resto de categorías una por una.
  144:         No depende de campañas ni de búsqueda.
  145:       */
  146:       for (const category of CATALOG_CATEGORIES) {
  147:         if (cancelled) break;
  148:         await ensureCategoryLoaded(category);
  149:       }
  150: 
  151:       if (!cancelled && mountedRef.current) {
  152:         setIsBootstrapping(false);
  153:       }
  154:     };
  155: 
  156:     run();
  157: 
  158:     return () => {
  159:       cancelled = true;
  160:     };
  161:   }, [activeCategory, ensureCategoryLoaded]);
  162: 
  163:   const data = useMemo(
  164:     () => flattenByCategory(productsByCategory),
  165:     [productsByCategory],
  166:   );
  167: 
  168:   const loadedCount = useMemo(
  169:     () =>
  170:       CATALOG_CATEGORIES.filter(
  171:         (category) => statusByCategory[category] === "loaded",
  172:       ).length,
  173:     [statusByCategory],
  174:   );
  175: 
  176:   const isFullCatalogLoaded = loadedCount === CATALOG_CATEGORIES.length;
  177: 
  178:   const activeCategoryStatus =
  179:     activeCategory === "todas" ? "loaded" : statusByCategory[activeCategory];
  180: 
  181:   const isCategoryLoading =
  182:     activeCategory !== "todas" && activeCategoryStatus === "loading";
  183: 
  184:   const isLoading = isBootstrapping && data.length === 0;
  185: 
  186:   return {
  187:     data,
  188:     productsByCategory,
  189:     statusByCategory,
  190:     activeCategoryStatus,
  191:     isCategoryLoading,
  192:     isLoading,
  193:     isFullCatalogLoaded,
  194:   };
  195: }

FILE: src\modules\catalog\hooks\useProducts.ts
============================================================

    1: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
    2: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    3: import {
    4:   getCachedCatalogSnapshot,
    5:   loadCatalogProgressive,
    6:   type CatalogCategory,
    7: } from "@/modules/catalog/services/fetchProducts";
    8: 
    9: export function useProducts(activeCategory: CatalogCategory = "todas") {
   10:   const initialSnapshot = getCachedCatalogSnapshot();
   11: 
   12:   const [data, setData] = useState<Product[]>(() => initialSnapshot.products);
   13:   const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(
   14:     () => initialSnapshot.products.length === 0,
   15:   );
   16:   const [isFullCatalogLoaded, setIsFullCatalogLoaded] = useState(
   17:     () => initialSnapshot.isFullCatalogLoaded,
   18:   );
   19: 
   20:   const dataRef = useRef(data);
   21: 
   22:   useEffect(() => {
   23:     dataRef.current = data;
   24:   }, [data]);
   25: 
   26:   useEffect(() => {
   27:     let cancelled = false;
   28: 
   29:     const hasPreviousProducts = dataRef.current.length > 0;
   30: 
   31:     /*
   32:       Regla UX:
   33:       - Skeleton solo en primera carga real.
   34:       - Si ya existe data, mantenemos pantalla mientras se actualiza.
   35:     */
   36:     setIsLoading(!hasPreviousProducts);
   37: 
   38:     loadCatalogProgressive(activeCategory, (products, fullLoaded) => {
   39:       if (cancelled) return;
   40: 
   41:       const hasIncomingProducts = products.length > 0;
   42: 
   43:       if (hasIncomingProducts) {
   44:         setData(products);
   45:       }
   46: 
   47:       setIsLoading(false);
   48: 
   49:       setIsFullCatalogLoaded((current) => current || fullLoaded);
   50:     });
   51: 
   52:     return () => {
   53:       cancelled = true;
   54:     };
   55:   }, [activeCategory]);
   56: 
   57:   return {
   58:     data,
   59:     isLoading,
   60:     isFullCatalogLoaded,
   61:   };
   62: }

FILE: src\modules\catalog\integrations\googleSheets\normalizeProduct.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import type { CatalogCategoryId } from "./sheetsConfig";
    3: import type { CsvRow } from "./fetchSheets";
    4: 
    5: export interface SheetProduct extends Product {
    6:   badges: string[];
    7:   campaigns: string[];
    8:   priority: number;
    9:   status: string;
   10:   updated_at: string;
   11: }
   12: 
   13: export type CampaignNameToIdMap = Record<string, string>;
   14: 
   15: function cleanText(value: unknown): string {
   16:   return String(value ?? "").trim();
   17: }
   18: 
   19: function parseNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
   20:   let cleaned = cleanText(value)
   21:     .replace(/S\/\.?/gi, "")
   22:     .replace(/\s/g, "");
   23: 
   24:   if (!cleaned) return null;
   25: 
   26:   if (cleaned.includes(",") && cleaned.includes(".")) {
   27:     cleaned = cleaned.replace(/,/g, "");
   28:   } else {
   29:     cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", ".");
   30:   }
   31: 
   32:   const num = Number(cleaned);
   33:   return Number.isFinite(num) ? num : null;
   34: }
   35: 
   36: function parseRequiredNumber(value: unknown): number {
   37:   return parseNumber(value) ?? 0;
   38: }
   39: 
   40: function parsePipeArray(value: unknown): string[] {
   41:   return cleanText(value)
   42:     .split("|")
   43:     .map((item) => item.trim())
   44:     .filter(Boolean);
   45: }
   46: 
   47: function parseCampaigns(
   48:   value: unknown,
   49:   campaignNameToIdMap: CampaignNameToIdMap,
   50: ): string[] {
   51:   return parsePipeArray(value)
   52:     .map((campaignName) => campaignNameToIdMap[campaignName])
   53:     .filter(Boolean);
   54: }
   55: 
   56: export function normalizeProduct(
   57:   row: CsvRow,
   58:   categoryFromConfig: CatalogCategoryId,
   59:   campaignNameToIdMap: CampaignNameToIdMap = {},
   60: ): SheetProduct {
   61:   return {
   62:     id: cleanText(row.id),
   63:     title: cleanText(row.title),
   64:     description: cleanText(row.description),
   65: 
   66:     category: categoryFromConfig,
   67: 
   68:     price_1: parseRequiredNumber(row.price_1),
   69:     price_3: parseNumber(row.price_3),
   70:     price_12: parseNumber(row.price_12),
   71:     price_50: parseNumber(row.price_50),
   72:     price_100: parseNumber(row.price_100),
   73: 
   74:     price_offer: parseNumber(row.price_offer),
   75: 
   76:     stock: parseNumber(row.stock),
   77: 
   78:     img: cleanText(row.cover || row.img),
   79:     gallery: cleanText(row.gallery || row.images),
   80: 
   81:     badges: parsePipeArray(row.badge),
   82:     campaigns: parseCampaigns(row.campaigns, campaignNameToIdMap),
   83: 
   84:     priority: parseRequiredNumber(row.priority),
   85:     status: cleanText(row.status),
   86:     updated_at: cleanText(row.updated_at),
   87:   };
   88: }

FILE: src\modules\catalog\integrations\googleSheets\validateProducts.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { SheetProduct } from "./normalizeProduct";
    2: 
    3: const PUBLIC_STATUSES = new Set([
    4:   "",
    5:   "publicado",
    6:   "publicada",
    7:   "published",
    8:   "activo",
    9:   "activa",
   10:   "active",
   11:   "preventa",
   12: ]);
   13: 
   14: function normalizeStatus(status: string | undefined) {
   15:   return String(status ?? "")
   16:     .trim()
   17:     .toLowerCase();
   18: }
   19: 
   20: export function validateProducts(products: SheetProduct[]) {
   21:   return products.filter((product) => {
   22:     if (!product.id) return false;
   23:     if (!product.title) return false;
   24:     if (!product.img) return false;
   25: 
   26:     const status = normalizeStatus(product.status);
   27: 
   28:     return PUBLIC_STATUSES.has(status);
   29:   });
   30: }

FILE: src\modules\catalog\providers\CatalogProvider.ts

    1: import type { Campaign, Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: /* =========================================================
    4:    TIPOS DEL PROVIDER
    5:    ========================================================= */
    6: 
    7: export type CatalogCategoryId = NonNullable<Product["category"]>;
    8: 
    9: /* =========================================================
   10:    CONTRATO DEL CATÁLOGO
   11:    ========================================================= */
   12: 
   13: export interface CatalogProvider {
   14:   getCategories(): readonly CatalogCategoryId[];
   15: 
   16:   loadCampaigns(): Promise<Campaign[]>;
   17: 
   18:   loadCategoryProducts(
   19:     category: CatalogCategoryId,

============================================================
FILE: src\modules\catalog\services\productService.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: import type { CatalogCategoryId } from "@/modules/catalog/providers/CatalogProvider";
    4: import { catalogProvider } from "@/modules/catalog/providers/DefaultCatalogProvider";
    5: 
    6: import { loadCatalogCampaigns } from "./campaignService";
    7: 
    8: /* =========================================================
    9:    TIPOS INTERNOS
   10:    ========================================================= */
   11: 
   12: type CategoryCacheEntry = {
   13:   savedAt: number;
   14:   items: Product[];
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export type CatalogCategory = CatalogCategoryId | "todas";
   18: 
   19: /* =========================================================
   20:    CONFIGURACIÓN
   21:    ========================================================= */
   22: 
   23: const CACHE_TTL = 1000 * 60 * 5;
   24: 
   25: /* =========================================================
   26:    CACHE EN MEMORIA
   27:    ========================================================= */
   28: 
   29: const memoryCache = new Map<CatalogCategoryId, CategoryCacheEntry>();
   30: 
   31: const pendingRequests = new Map<CatalogCategoryId, Promise<Product[]>>();
   32: 
   33: /* =========================================================
   34:    HELPERS
   35:    ========================================================= */
   36: 
   37: const productKey = (category: string) => `jung_catalog_v3_${category}`;
   38: 
   39: const now = () => Date.now();
   40: 
   41: const isFresh = (savedAt: number) => now() - savedAt <= CACHE_TTL;
   42: 
   43: export const getCatalogCategories = (): CatalogCategoryId[] => [
   44:   ...catalogProvider.getCategories(),
   45: ];
   46: 
   47: const sortProducts = (items: Product[]) =>
   48:   [...items].sort((a, b) => (b.priority ?? 0) - (a.priority ?? 0));
   49: 
   50: const flattenByCategory = (
   51:   byCategory: Partial<Record<CatalogCategoryId, Product[]>>,
   52: ) => sortProducts(Object.values(byCategory).flatMap((items) => items ?? []));
   53: 
   54: /* =========================================================
   55:    CACHE LOCAL DE PRODUCTOS
   56:    ========================================================= */
   57: 
   58: function readStorageCache(category: CatalogCategoryId): Product[] | null {
   59:   if (typeof window === "undefined") {
   60:     return null;
   61:   }
   62: 
   63:   try {
   64:     const raw = localStorage.getItem(productKey(category));
   65: 
   66:     if (!raw) {
   67:       return null;
   68:     }
   69: 
   70:     const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as CategoryCacheEntry;
   71: 
   72:     if (!parsed?.savedAt || !isFresh(parsed.savedAt)) {
   73:       return null;
   74:     }
   75: 
   76:     if (!Array.isArray(parsed.items)) {
   77:       return null;
   78:     }
   79: 
   80:     memoryCache.set(category, {
   81:       savedAt: parsed.savedAt,
   82:       items: parsed.items,
   83:     });
   84: 
   85:     return parsed.items;
   86:   } catch {
   87:     return null;
   88:   }
   89: }
   90: 
   91: export function readCachedCategoryProducts(
   92:   category: CatalogCategoryId,
   93: ): Product[] | null {
   94:   const memoryEntry = memoryCache.get(category);
   95: 
   96:   if (memoryEntry && isFresh(memoryEntry.savedAt)) {
   97:     return sortProducts(memoryEntry.items);
   98:   }
   99: 
  100:   if (memoryEntry && !isFresh(memoryEntry.savedAt)) {
  101:     memoryCache.delete(category);
  102:   }
  103: 
  104:   const storageItems = readStorageCache(category);
  105: 
  106:   return storageItems ? sortProducts(storageItems) : null;
  107: }
  108: 
  109: function writeCache(category: CatalogCategoryId, items: Product[]): void {
  110:   const entry: CategoryCacheEntry = {
  111:     savedAt: now(),
  112:     items,
  113:   };
  114: 
  115:   memoryCache.set(category, entry);
  116: 
  117:   if (typeof window === "undefined") {
  118:     return;
  119:   }
  120: 
  121:   try {
  122:     localStorage.setItem(productKey(category), JSON.stringify(entry));
  123:   } catch {
  124:     // Si localStorage falla, se conserva el cache en memoria.
  125:   }
  126: }
  127: 
  128: /* =========================================================
  129:    SNAPSHOT DEL CATÁLOGO
  130:    ========================================================= */
  131: 
  132: export function getCachedCatalogSnapshot() {
  133:   const categories = getCatalogCategories();
  134: 
  135:   const byCategory: Partial<Record<CatalogCategoryId, Product[]>> = {};
  136: 
  137:   categories.forEach((category) => {
  138:     const cached = readCachedCategoryProducts(category);
  139: 
  140:     if (cached) {
  141:       byCategory[category] = cached;
  142:     }
  143:   });
  144: 
  145:   const loadedCategories = categories.filter(
  146:     (category) => byCategory[category],
  147:   );
  148: 
  149:   const isFullCatalogLoaded = loadedCategories.length === categories.length;
  150: 
  151:   return {
  152:     byCategory,
  153:     products: flattenByCategory(byCategory),
  154:     loadedCategories,
  155:     isFullCatalogLoaded,
  156:   };
  157: }
  158: 
  159: /* =========================================================
  160:    CARGA DESDE EL PROVIDER
  161:    ========================================================= */
  162: 
  163: async function fetchCategoryProducts(
  164:   category: CatalogCategoryId,
  165: ): Promise<Product[]> {
  166:   const campaigns =
  167:     await loadCatalogCampaigns({
  168:       includeInactive: true,
  169:     });
  170: 
  171:   const products =
  172:     await catalogProvider.loadCategoryProducts(
  173:       category,
  174:       campaigns,
  175:     );
  176: 
  177:   const sortedProducts =
  178:     sortProducts(products);
  179: 
  180:   writeCache(
  181:     category,
  182:     sortedProducts,
  183:   );
  184: 
  185:   return sortedProducts;
  186: }
  187: /* =========================================================
  188:    API DE CARGA POR CATEGORÍA
  189:    ========================================================= */
  190: 
  191: export async function loadCategoryProducts(
  192:   category: CatalogCategoryId,
  193:   options: { forceRefresh?: boolean } = {},
  194: ): Promise<Product[]> {
  195:   if (!options.forceRefresh) {
  196:     const cached = readCachedCategoryProducts(category);
  197: 
  198:     if (cached) {
  199:       return cached;
  200:     }
  201:   }
  202: 
  203:   const pendingRequest = pendingRequests.get(category);
  204: 
  205:   if (pendingRequest) {
  206:     return pendingRequest;
  207:   }
  208: 
  209:   const request = fetchCategoryProducts(category).finally(() => {
  210:     pendingRequests.delete(category);
  211:   });
  212: 
  213:   pendingRequests.set(category, request);
  214: 
  215:   return request;
  216: }
  217: 
  218: export async function loadAllProducts(): Promise<Product[]> {
  219:   const categories = getCatalogCategories();
  220: 
  221:   const results = await Promise.all(
  222:     categories.map((category) =>
  223:       loadCategoryProducts(category).catch((error: unknown) => {
  224:         console.error(`Error en categoría "${category}":`, error);
  225: 
  226:         return [];
  227:       }),
  228:     ),
  229:   );
  230: 
  231:   return sortProducts(results.flat());
  232: }
  233: 
  234: /* =========================================================
  235:    CARGA PROGRESIVA
  236:    ========================================================= */
  237: 
  238: export async function loadCatalogProgressive(
  239:   activeCategory: CatalogCategory,
  240:   onUpdate: (products: Product[], isFullCatalogLoaded: boolean) => void,
  241: ): Promise<void> {
  242:   const categories = getCatalogCategories();
  243: 
  244:   const preferredCategory = activeCategory !== "todas" ? activeCategory : null;
  245: 
  246:   const categoryOrder = preferredCategory
  247:     ? [
  248:         preferredCategory,
  249:         ...categories.filter((category) => category !== preferredCategory),
  250:       ]
  251:     : categories;
  252: 
  253:   const byCategory = getCachedCatalogSnapshot().byCategory;
  254: 
  255:   onUpdate(
  256:     flattenByCategory(byCategory),
  257:     Object.keys(byCategory).length === categories.length,
  258:   );
  259: 
  260:   for (const category of categoryOrder) {
  261:     if (byCategory[category]) {
  262:       continue;
  263:     }
  264: 
  265:     try {
  266:       byCategory[category] = await loadCategoryProducts(category);
  267:     } catch (error: unknown) {
  268:       console.error(`Error cargando "${category}":`, error);
  269: 
  270:       byCategory[category] = [];
  271:     }
  272: 
  273:     onUpdate(
  274:       flattenByCategory(byCategory),
  275:       Object.keys(byCategory).length === categories.length,
  276:     );
  277:   }
  278: }
  279: 
  280: 
  281:

FILE: src\modules\integrations\quality\validators\ImageRule.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import type { QualityIssue } from "../models";
    3: import type { QualityRule } from "../contracts/QualityRule";
    4: 
    5: const isValidImage = (url: string) =>
    6:   /^https?:\/\/.+\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)(\?.*)?$/i.test(url) || url.includes("dl.dropboxusercontent.com");
    7: 
    8: export const ImageRule: QualityRule<Product> = {
    9:   key: "image",
   10:   name: "Imagen principal",
   11:   description: "Valida que el producto tenga una imagen principal pública y compatible.",
   12:   category: "media",
   13:   weight: 20,
   14:   required: true,
   15:   enabled: true,
   16: 
   17:   validate(product) {
   18:     const issues: QualityIssue[] = [];
   19:     const img = String(product.img || "").trim();
   20: 
   21:     if (!img) {
   22:       issues.push({ level: "error", code: "IMAGE_EMPTY", field: "img", message: "El producto no tiene imagen principal." });
   23:       return issues;
   24:     }
   25: 
   26:     if (!img.startsWith("http")) {
   27:       issues.push({ level: "error", code: "IMAGE_NOT_PUBLIC_URL", field: "img", message: "La imagen debe ser una URL pública." });
   28:     }
   29: 
   30:     if (!isValidImage(img)) {
   31:       issues.push({ level: "warning", code: "IMAGE_FORMAT_REVIEW", field: "img", message: "La imagen no parece tener un formato estándar jpg, png o webp." });
   32:     }
   33: 
   34:     return issues;
   35:   },
   36: };

FILE: src\modules\integrations\quality\validators\PriceRule.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import type { QualityIssue } from "../models";
    3: import type { QualityRule } from "../contracts/QualityRule";
    4: 
    5: export const PriceRule: QualityRule<Product> = {
    6:   key: "price",
    7:   name: "Precio válido",
    8:   description: "Valida que el producto tenga un precio positivo y exportable.",
    9:   category: "pricing",
   10:   weight: 20,
   11:   required: true,
   12:   enabled: true,
   13: 
   14:   validate(product) {
   15:     const issues: QualityIssue[] = [];
   16:     const price = Number(product.price_offer || product.price_1 || 0);
   17: 
   18:     if (!price || price <= 0) {
   19:       issues.push({ level: "error", code: "PRICE_INVALID", field: "price_1", message: "El producto debe tener un precio mayor a cero." });
   20:     }
   21: 
   22:     if (price > 99999) {
   23:       issues.push({ level: "warning", code: "PRICE_TOO_HIGH", field: "price_1", message: "El precio parece demasiado alto. Revisar antes de exportar." });
   24:     }
   25: 
   26:     return issues;
   27:   },
   28: };
