FILE: src\modules\product-detail\components\ProductGallery.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
    2: import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, ZoomIn } from "lucide-react";
    3: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    4: import { getProductMedia } from "@/shared/lib/productMedia";
    5: import { getBadgePresentation, sortBadges } from "@/shared/config/badgeRules";
    6: 
    7: interface ProductGalleryProps {
    8:   product: Product;
    9:   available: boolean;
   10:   onZoom: (src: string, title: string) => void;
   11: }
   12: 
   13: export function ProductGallery({
   14:   product,
   15:   available,
   16:   onZoom,
   17: }: ProductGalleryProps) {
   18:   const media = useMemo(() => getProductMedia(product), [product]);
   19:   const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   20:   const [heroLoaded, setHeroLoaded] = useState(false);
   21:   const touchStartX = useRef<number | null>(null);
   22:   const activeMedia = media[activeIndex] ?? media[0];
   23:   const hasMany = media.length > 1;
   24: 
   25:   const maxVisibleThumbs = 5;
   26:   const visibleMedia = media.slice(0, maxVisibleThumbs);
   27:   const hiddenCount = Math.max(media.length - maxVisibleThumbs, 0);
   28: 
   29:   const goPrev = () =>
   30:     setActiveIndex((i) => (i === 0 ? media.length - 1 : i - 1));
   31:   const goNext = () =>
   32:     setActiveIndex((i) => (i === media.length - 1 ? 0 : i + 1));
   33: 
   34:   useEffect(() => {
   35:     setActiveIndex(0);
   36:   }, [product.id]);
   37: 
   38:   const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
   39:     touchStartX.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
   40:   };
   41: 
   42:   const handleTouchEnd = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
   43:     if (!hasMany || touchStartX.current === null) return;
   44: 
   45:     const diff = touchStartX.current - e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
   46: 
   47:     if (Math.abs(diff) > 45) {
   48:       diff > 0 ? goNext() : goPrev();
   49:     }
   50: 
   51:     touchStartX.current = null;
   52:   };
   53: 
   54:   return (
   55:     <div className="relative flex flex-col gap-3 md:grid md:grid-cols-[96px_minmax(0,1fr)] xl:grid-cols-[100px_minmax(0,1fr)] md:gap-3 xl:gap-5">
   56:       {hasMany && (
   57:         <div className="order-2 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 md:order-1 md:flex-col md:max-h-[560px] md:overflow-x-visible md:overflow-y-auto md:pb-0">
   58:           {visibleMedia.map((item, index) => {
   59:             const isActive = index === activeIndex;
   60: 
   61:             return (
   62:               <button
   63:                 key={item.id}
   64:                 type="button"
   65:                 onMouseEnter={() => {
   66:                   if (activeIndex !== index) {
   67:                     setActiveIndex(index);
   68:                   }
   69:                 }}
   70:                 onClick={() => setActiveIndex(index)}
   71:                 className={[
   72:                   "relative h-20 w-16 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border bg-white transition-all duration-200 md:h-[100px] md:w-[75px] xl:h-[100px] xl:w-[75px]",
   73:                   isActive
   74:                     ? "z-10 scale-105 border-[#1d8299] opacity-100 ring-2 ring-[#1d8299]/25 shadow-xl"
   75:                     : "border-[#e2e8f0] opacity-70 hover:scale-[1.02] hover:opacity-100",
   76:                 ].join(" ")}
   77:               >
   78:                 <img
   79:                   src={item.thumb || item.src}
   80:                   alt={item.alt}
   81:                   className="h-full w-full object-cover object-center"
   82:                 />
   83:               </button>
   84:             );
   85:           })}
   86: 
   87:           {hiddenCount > 0 && (
   88:             <button
   89:               type="button"
   90:               onClick={() => onZoom(maxVisibleThumbs)}
   91:               className="relative h-20 w-16 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl md:h-[100px] md:w-[75px] xl:h-[100px] xl:w-[75px]"
   92:             >
   93:               <img
   94:                 src={
   95:                   media[maxVisibleThumbs]?.thumb || media[maxVisibleThumbs]?.src
   96:                 }
   97:                 alt="Ver todas"
   98:                 className="h-full w-full object-cover object-center brightness-50"
   99:               />
  100: 
  101:               <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-black/20 text-white">
  102:                 <span className="text-lg font-black">+{hiddenCount}</span>
  103:                 <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold">Ver todas</span>
  104:               </div>
  105:             </button>
  106:           )}
  107:         </div>
  108:       )}
  109: 
  110:       <div
  111:         className="order-1 relative aspect-[4/5] sm:aspect-[3/4] overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-[#e2e8f0] bg-white shadow-[0_24px_60px_rgba(15,23,42,0.16)] group cursor-zoom-in md:order-2 lg:min-h-[560px] xl:min-h-[620px]"
  112:         onClick={() => onZoom(activeIndex)}
  113:         onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
  114:         onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
  115:       >
  116:         <img
  117:           key={activeMedia.id}
  118:           src={activeMedia.src}
  119:           alt={activeMedia.alt}
  120:           onLoad={() => setHeroLoaded(true)}
  121:           className={[
  122:             "h-full w-full object-cover object-center transition-all duration-300 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.02]",
  123:             heroLoaded ? "scale-100 opacity-100" : "scale-[0.985] opacity-0",
  124:             !available ? "grayscale-[50%]" : "",
  125:           ].join(" ")}
  126:         />
  127: 
  128:         {product.badges && product.badges.length > 0 && (
  129:           <div className="absolute left-4 top-4 z-10 flex max-w-[75%] flex-col items-start gap-2">
  130:             {sortBadges(product.badges)
  131:               .slice(0, 3)
  132:               .map((badge, index) => {
  133:                 const presentation = getBadgePresentation(badge);
  134: 
  135:                 return (
  136:                   <div
  137:                     key={`${product.id}-detail-image-badge-${index}`}
  138:                     className={[
  139:                       "rounded-full border border-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-semibold leading-tight tracking-normal shadow-md backdrop-blur-sm md:text-[11px]",
  140:                       presentation.className,
  141:                       presentation.animation,
  142:                     ].join(" ")}
  143:                   >
  144:                     {badge}
  145:                   </div>
  146:                 );
  147:               })}
  148:           </div>
  149:         )}
  150: 
  151:         {hasMany && (
  152:           <>
  153:             <button
  154:               type="button"
  155:               onClick={(e) => {
  156:                 e.stopPropagation();
  157:                 goPrev();
  158:               }}
  159:               className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 z-20 hidden h-11 w-11 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/90 text-slate-700 opacity-0 shadow-lg backdrop-blur-md transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:bg-white group-hover:opacity-100 md:flex"
  160:               aria-label="Imagen anterior"
  161:             >
  162:               <ChevronLeft className="h-6 w-6" />
  163:             </button>
  164: 
  165:             <button
  166:               type="button"
  167:               onClick={(e) => {
  168:                 e.stopPropagation();
  169:                 goNext();
  170:               }}
  171:               className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 z-20 hidden h-11 w-11 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/90 text-slate-700 opacity-0 shadow-lg backdrop-blur-md transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:bg-white group-hover:opacity-100 md:flex"
  172:               aria-label="Imagen siguiente"
  173:             >
  174:               <ChevronRight className="h-6 w-6" />
  175:             </button>
  176:           </>
  177:         )}
  178: 
  179:         <div className="absolute bottom-4 right-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#e2e8f0] bg-white/90 p-2.5 text-[#334155] shadow-lg backdrop-blur-md transition-all group-hover:scale-105">
  180:           <ZoomIn className="h-5 w-5" />
  181:         </div>
  182: 
  183:         {hasMany && (
  184:           <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-4 rounded-2xl bg-black/45 px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-black text-white backdrop-blur-md">
  185:             📷 {activeIndex + 1} de {media.length}
  186:           </div>
  187:         )}
  188:       </div>
  189:     </div>
  190:   );

FILE: src\modules\product-detail\components\ProductTierProgress.tsx
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: interface Props{
    4:   product:Product;
    5:   effectiveQty:number;
    6:   nextTier:{qty:number;price:number}|null;
    7: }
    8: 
    9: export function ProductTierProgress({product,effectiveQty,nextTier}:Props){
   10:   const tiers=[
   11:     {qty:1,price:product.price_1},
   12:     {qty:3,price:product.price_3},
   13:     {qty:12,price:product.price_12},
   14:     {qty:50,price:product.price_50},
   15:     {qty:100,price:product.price_100},
   16:   ].filter(t=>typeof t.price==="number"&&Number.isFinite(t.price)&&t.price>0);
   17: 
   18:   const bestTarget=tiers.at(-1)?.qty??1;
   19:   const activeTiers=tiers.map(t=>t.qty);
   20:   const currentTierIndex=activeTiers.reduce((acc,qty,i)=>effectiveQty>=qty?i:acc,0);
   21:   const nextTierIndex=activeTiers.findIndex(qty=>effectiveQty<qty);
   22:   const nextIndex=nextTierIndex===-1?activeTiers.length-1:nextTierIndex;
   23:   const prevQty=activeTiers[currentTierIndex]??1;
   24:   const nextQty=activeTiers[nextIndex]??prevQty;
   25:   const segmentBase=activeTiers.length>1?100/(activeTiers.length-1):100;
   26:   const segmentProgress=nextQty>prevQty?((effectiveQty-prevQty)/(nextQty-prevQty))*segmentBase:0;
   27:   const rawProgress=Math.min((currentTierIndex*segmentBase)+segmentProgress,100);
   28:   const progress=effectiveQty>0?Math.max(rawProgress,10):0;
   29:   const unlocked=effectiveQty>=bestTarget;
   30:   const targetQty=nextTier?.qty??bestTarget;
   31:   const missingQty=Math.max(targetQty-effectiveQty,0);
   32: 
   33:   if(tiers.length<=1)return null;
   34: 
   35:   return(
   36:      <div className="mt-1">
   37:       <div className="mb-2 flex justify-end text-[12px] font-black">
   38:         <span className={unlocked?"text-emerald-600":"text-orange-500"}>
   39:             {effectiveQty}/{bestTarget}
   40:         </span>
   41:       </div>
   42: 
   43:       <div className="h-4 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200 shadow-inner">
   44:         <div className={["h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(34,197,94,.25)]",unlocked?"bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-green-600":"bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 via-amber-400 to-emerald-500"].join(" ")} style={{width:`${progress}%`}}/>
   45:       </div>
   46: 
   47:       <p className="mt-2 text-center text-[14px] font-bold leading-snug text-slate-600">
   48:         {unlocked?(<>🎉 Mejor precio desbloqueado</>):nextTier?(<>🚀 Agrega <span className="text-[#1d8299]">{missingQty}</span> más y baja a <span className="text-[#1d8299]">S/{nextTier.price.toFixed(2)}</span> c/u</>):(<>✅ Ya tienes el mejor precio disponible</>)}
   49:       </p>
   50:     </div>
   51:   );
   52: }

FILE: src\modules\product-detail\components\ProductTierSelector.tsx
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: import { PRICE_TIERS } from "@/shared/config/priceTiers";
    3: 
    4: interface ProductTierSelectorProps {
    5:   product: Product;
    6:   effectiveQty: number;
    7:   onSelectQty: (qty: number) => void;
    8: }
    9: 
   10: export function ProductTierSelector({
   11:   product,
   12:   effectiveQty,
   13:   onSelectQty,
   14: }: ProductTierSelectorProps) {
   15:   return (
   16:     <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center md:justify-start gap-2">
   17:       {PRICE_TIERS.map((tier) => {
   18:         const value = product[tier.key];
   19: 
   20:         if (!value) return null;
   21: 
   22:         const active =
   23:           (tier.qty === 1 && effectiveQty < 3) ||
   24:           (tier.qty === 3 && effectiveQty >= 3 && effectiveQty < 12) ||
   25:           (tier.qty === 12 && effectiveQty >= 12 && effectiveQty < 50) ||
   26:           (tier.qty === 50 && effectiveQty >= 50 && effectiveQty < 100) ||
   27:           (tier.qty === 100 && effectiveQty >= 100);
   28: 
   29:         const tierStyle = active
   30:           ? "bg-[#1d8299] text-white border-[#1d8299] shadow-md"
   31:           : tier.qty === 3
   32:             ? "bg-[#fff7e6] text-[#f59e0b] border-[#f6d28b] hover:bg-[#fff1cc]"
   33:             : tier.qty === 12
   34:               ? "bg-[#fff0f7] text-[#f286be] border-[#f6bfdc] hover:bg-[#ffe3f1]"
   35:               : tier.qty === 50
   36:                 ? "bg-[#f3efff] text-[#8b5cf6] border-[#d8ccff] hover:bg-[#ebe3ff]"
   37:                 : tier.qty === 100
   38:                   ? "bg-[#f1f5f9] text-[#0f172a] border-[#cbd5e1] hover:bg-[#e2e8f0]"
   39:                   : "bg-white text-[#1d8299] border-[#d8e2ed] hover:bg-[#f0f8fa]";
   40: 
   41:         return (
   42:           <button
   43:             key={tier.key}
   44:             type="button"
   45:             onClick={()=>onSelectQty(tier.qty)}
   46:             className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-200 min-w-[96px] shadow-sm cursor-pointer ${tierStyle} ${active?"scale-[1.04]":"hover:scale-[1.03]"}`}
   47:           >
   48:             <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap">
   49:               <span className="text-[13px] font-black tracking-wide">
   50:                 {tier.label}
   51:               </span>
   52: 
   53:               <span className="text-[13px] md:text-sm font-black">
   54:                 S/{value.toFixed(2)}
   55:               </span>
   56:             </div>
   57:           </button>
   58:         );
   59:       })}
   60:     </div>
   61:   );
   62: }

FILE: src\modules\product-detail\hooks\useProductCard.ts
============================================================

    1: import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
    2: import type { CartItem, Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    3: import { isProductAvailable } from "@/modules/catalog/utils/products";
    4: import { buildWhatsappMessage } from "@/modules/product-detail/utils/buildWhatsappMessage";
    5: 
    6: export function useProductCard(
    7:   p: Product,
    8:   cart: CartItem[],
    9:   onAddToCart: (product: Product) => void,
   10: ) {
   11:   const navigate = useNavigate();
   12: 
   13:   const status = (p.status || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   14: 
   15:   const isPublicado = status === "publicado";
   16:   const isPreventa = status === "preventa";
   17:   const isAgotado = status === "agotado";
   18: 
   19:   const available = isProductAvailable(p) && isPublicado;
   20: 
   21:   const isOutOfStock =
   22:     !isPreventa && !isAgotado && !!p.price_1 && p.stock === 0;
   23: 
   24:   const showWhatsAppButton = isPreventa || isAgotado;
   25: 
   26:   const cartItem = cart.find((item) => item.id === p.id);
   27:   const qtyInCart = cartItem?.qty ?? 0;
   28:   const isInCart = qtyInCart > 0;
   29: 
   30:   function goToDetail() {
   31:     navigate(`/catalogo/producto.html?id=${p.id}&cat=${p.category}`);
   32:   }
   33: 
   34:   function handleAdd() {
   35:     if (!available || isPreventa || isAgotado || isOutOfStock) return;
   36:     onAddToCart(p);
   37:   }
   38: 
   39:   function handleWhatsApp() {
   40:     const message = isAgotado
   41:       ? [
   42:           "Hola Wooly, quiero consultar reposición de este producto:",
   43:           "",
   44:           `Producto: ${p.title}`,
   45:           `Código: ${p.id}`,
   46:           `Categoría: ${p.category}`,
   47:           "",
   48:           `Link: ${window.location.href}`,
   49:         ]
   50:           .filter(Boolean)
   51:           .join("\n")
   52:       : buildWhatsappMessage(p, isPreventa, isOutOfStock);
   53: 
   54:     window.open(
   55:       `https://wa.me/51936188636?text=${encodeURIComponent(message)}`,
   56:       "_blank",
   57:     );
   58:   }
   59: 
   60:   return {
   61:     available,
   62:     isPublicado,
   63:     isPreventa,
   64:     isAgotado,
   65:     isOutOfStock,
   66:     showWhatsAppButton,
   67:     qtyInCart,
   68:     isInCart,
   69:     goToDetail,
   70:     handleAdd,
   71:     handleWhatsApp,
   72:   };
   73: }

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";

    3: export function buildWhatsappMessage(
    4:  product:Product,
    5:  isPreventa:boolean,
    6:  isOutOfStock:boolean
    7: ){
    8: 
    9: if(isPreventa){
   10:

   12: "Hola, quiero información sobre este producto en preventa:",
   13: "",
   14: `ID: ${product.id}`,
   15: `Producto: ${product.title}`,

   17: ].join("%0A");
   18: 
   19: }
   20: 
   21: if(isOutOfStock){
   22: 
   23: return[
   24: "Hola, quiero pedir reposición de este producto:",
   25: "",
   26: `ID: ${product.id}`,
   27: `Producto: ${product.title}`,
   28: `Categoría: ${product.category}`
   29: ].join("%0A");
   30: 
   31: }
   32: 
   33: return[
   34: "Hola, quiero más información sobre este producto:",
   35: "",
   36: `ID: ${product.id}`,
   37: `Producto: ${product.title}`,
   38: `Categoría: ${product.category}`
   39: ].join("%0A");
   40: 
   41: }

FILE: src\shared\components\media\ImageZoomModal.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
    2: import { X, RotateCcw, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
    3: import type { ProductMedia } from "@/shared/lib/productMedia";
    4: 
    5: interface ImageZoomModalProps {
    6:   media?: ProductMedia[];
    7:   initialIndex?: number;
    8:   open?: boolean;
    9:   src?: string | null;
   10:   title: string;
   11:   onClose: () => void;
   12: }
   13: 
   14: const clamp = (value: number, min: number, max: number) =>
   15:   Math.max(min, Math.min(value, max));
   16: 
   17: const getTouchDistance = (touches: React.TouchList) => {
   18:   const a = touches[0];
   19:   const b = touches[1];
   20:   return Math.hypot(a.clientX - b.clientX, a.clientY - b.clientY);
   21: };
   22: 
   23: export function ImageZoomModal({
   24:   media = [],
   25:   initialIndex = 0,
   26:   open,
   27:   src,
   28:   title,
   29:   onClose,
   30: }: ImageZoomModalProps) {
   31:   const normalizedMedia =
   32:     media.length > 0
   33:       ? media
   34:       : src
   35:         ? [
   36:             {
   37:               id: "legacy-image",
   38:               type: "image" as const,
   39:               src,
   40:               thumb: src,
   41:               alt: title,
   42:               order: 1,
   43:             },
   44:           ]
   45:         : [];
   46: 
   47:   const isOpen = open ?? !!src;
   48: 
   49:   const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(initialIndex);
   50:   const [imageLoaded, setImageLoaded] = useState(false);
   51: 
   52:   const [scale, setScale] = useState(1);
   53:   const [pos, setPos] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
   54: 
   55:   const activeMedia = normalizedMedia[activeIndex] ?? normalizedMedia[0];
   56:   const hasMany = normalizedMedia.length > 1;
   57: 
   58:   const mouseRef = useRef({ panning: false, startX: 0, startY: 0 });
   59:   const touchRef = useRef({
   60:     panning: false,
   61:     pinching: false,
   62:     swiping: false,
   63:     startX: 0,
   64:     startY: 0,
   65:     startDistance: 0,
   66:     startScale: 1,
   67:   });
   68:   const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   69:   const historyPushedRef = useRef(false);
   70:   const closingFromPopStateRef = useRef(false);
   71: 
   72:   const reset = useCallback(() => {
   73:     setScale(1);
   74:     setPos({ x: 0, y: 0 });
   75:   }, []);
   76: 
   77:   const goPrev = useCallback(() => {
   78:     setActiveIndex((index) =>
   79:       index === 0 ? normalizedMedia.length - 1 : index - 1,
   80:     );
   81:     reset();
   82:   }, [normalizedMedia.length, reset]);
   83: 
   84:   const goNext = useCallback(() => {
   85:     setActiveIndex((index) =>
   86:       index === normalizedMedia.length - 1 ? 0 : index + 1,
   87:     );
   88:     reset();
   89:   }, [normalizedMedia.length, reset]);
   90: 
   91:   const closeModal = useCallback(() => {
   92:     if (historyPushedRef.current && !closingFromPopStateRef.current) {
   93:       historyPushedRef.current = false;
   94:       window.history.back();
   95:       return;
   96:     }
   97: 
   98:     onClose();
   99:   }, [onClose]);
  100: 
  101:   useEffect(() => {
  102:     if (!open) return;
  103:     setActiveIndex(initialIndex);
  104:     reset();
  105:   }, [open, initialIndex, reset]);
  106: 
  107:   useEffect(() => {
  108:     setImageLoaded(false);
  109:   }, [activeIndex]);
  110: 
  111:   useEffect(() => {
  112:     if (!open) return;
  113: 
  114:     window.history.pushState({ imageZoom: true }, "");
  115:     historyPushedRef.current = true;
  116:     closingFromPopStateRef.current = false;
  117: 
  118:     const handlePopState = () => {
  119:       closingFromPopStateRef.current = true;
  120:       historyPushedRef.current = false;
  121:       onClose();
  122:     };
  123: 
  124:     window.addEventListener("popstate", handlePopState);
  125: 
  126:     return () => {
  127:       window.removeEventListener("popstate", handlePopState);
  128:     };
  129:   }, [open, onClose]);
  130: 
  131:   useEffect(() => {
  132:     if (!open) return;
  133: 
  134:     const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
  135:       if (e.key === "Escape") closeModal();
  136: 
  137:       if (hasMany && scale <= 1) {
  138:         if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") goPrev();
  139:         if (e.key === "ArrowRight") goNext();
  140:       }
  141:     };
  142: 
  143:     window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  144: 
  145:     return () => {
  146:       window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  147:     };
  148:   }, [open, closeModal, hasMany, scale, goPrev, goNext]);
  149: 
  150:   useEffect(() => {
  151:     const el = containerRef.current;
  152:     if (!el || !open) return;
  153: 
  154:     const handler = (e: WheelEvent) => {
  155:       e.preventDefault();
  156: 
  157:       setScale((current) => {
  158:         const next = e.deltaY < 0 ? current * 1.12 : current / 1.12;
  159:         const clamped = clamp(next, 1, 5);
  160: 
  161:         if (clamped === 1) setPos({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  162: 
  163:         return clamped;
  164:       });
  165:     };
  166: 
  167:     el.addEventListener("wheel", handler, { passive: false });
  168: 
  169:     return () => {
  170:       el.removeEventListener("wheel", handler);
  171:     };
  172:   }, [open]);
  173: 
  174:   if (!isOpen || !activeMedia) return null;
  175: 
  176:   const handleMouseDown = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
  177:     if (scale <= 1) return;
  178: 
  179:     e.preventDefault();
  180: 
  181:     mouseRef.current = {
  182:       panning: true,
  183:       startX: e.clientX - pos.x,
  184:       startY: e.clientY - pos.y,
  185:     };
  186:   };
  187: 
  188:   const handleMouseMove = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
  189:     if (!mouseRef.current.panning) return;
  190: 
  191:     setPos({
  192:       x: e.clientX - mouseRef.current.startX,
  193:       y: e.clientY - mouseRef.current.startY,
  194:     });
  195:   };
  196: 
  197:   const handleMouseUp = () => {
  198:     mouseRef.current.panning = false;
  199:   };
  200: 
  201:   const handleDoubleClick = () => {
  202:     if (scale > 1) {
  203:       reset();
  204:       return;
  205:     }
  206: 
  207:     setScale(2.4);
  208:   };
  209: 
  210:   const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
  211:     if (e.touches.length === 2) {
  212:       touchRef.current = {
  213:         ...touchRef.current,
  214:         pinching: true,
  215:         panning: false,
  216:         swiping: false,
  217:         startDistance: getTouchDistance(e.touches),
  218:         startScale: scale,
  219:       };
  220:       return;
  221:     }
  222: 
  223:     if (e.touches.length === 1) {
  224:       const touch = e.touches[0];
  225: 
  226:       touchRef.current = {
  227:         ...touchRef.current,
  228:         pinching: false,
  229:         panning: scale > 1,
  230:         swiping: scale <= 1,
  231:         startX: scale > 1 ? touch.clientX - pos.x : touch.clientX,
  232:         startY: scale > 1 ? touch.clientY - pos.y : touch.clientY,
  233:       };
  234:     }
  235:   };
  236: 
  237:   const handleTouchMove = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
  238:     if (e.touches.length === 2 && touchRef.current.pinching) {
  239:       e.preventDefault();
  240: 
  241:       const distance = getTouchDistance(e.touches);
  242:       const nextScale = clamp(
  243:         (distance / touchRef.current.startDistance) *
  244:           touchRef.current.startScale,
  245:         1,
  246:         5,
  247:       );
  248: 
  249:       setScale(nextScale);
  250: 
  251:       if (nextScale === 1) setPos({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  252: 
  253:       return;
  254:     }
  255: 
  256:     if (e.touches.length === 1 && touchRef.current.panning && scale > 1) {
  257:       e.preventDefault();
  258: 
  259:       const touch = e.touches[0];
  260: 
  261:       setPos({
  262:         x: touch.clientX - touchRef.current.startX,
  263:         y: touch.clientY - touchRef.current.startY,
  264:       });
  265:     }
  266:   };
  267: 
  268:   const handleTouchEnd = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
  269:     if (hasMany && touchRef.current.swiping && scale <= 1) {
  270:       const endX = e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX ?? touchRef.current.startX;
  271:       const diff = touchRef.current.startX - endX;
  272: 
  273:       if (Math.abs(diff) > 45) {
  274:         diff > 0 ? goNext() : goPrev();
  275:       }
  276:     }
  277: 
  278:     touchRef.current.panning = false;
  279:     touchRef.current.pinching = false;
  280:     touchRef.current.swiping = false;
  281: 
  282:     if (scale <= 1.03) reset();
  283:   };
  284: 
  285:   return (
  286:     <div
  287:       className="fixed inset-0 z-[2500] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/95 p-2 backdrop-blur-md transition-opacity duration-300 md:p-4"
  288:       onClick={(e) => {
  289:         if (e.target === e.currentTarget) closeModal();
  290:       }}
  291:       onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}
  292:       onMouseUp={handleMouseUp}
  293:       onMouseLeave={handleMouseUp}
  294:     >
  295:       <div
  296:         ref={containerRef}
  297:         className="relative flex aspect-[3/4] h-auto max-h-[92vh] w-[94vw] max-w-[430px] touch-none select-none items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-slate-900 shadow-2xl ring-1 ring-white/10 md:h-[78vh] md:w-full md:max-w-5xl md:aspect-auto"
  298:         onDoubleClick={handleDoubleClick}
  299:         onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
  300:         onTouchMove={handleTouchMove}
  301:         onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
  302:       >
  303:         <div className="absolute left-0 top-0 z-30 flex min-h-16 w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-gradient-to-b from-black/75 via-black/45 to-transparent px-4 py-3 md:min-h-20 md:px-5">
  304:           <h2 className="line-clamp-2 max-w-[68%] text-sm font-black leading-tight text-white md:max-w-[75%] md:text-lg">
  305:             {title}
  306:           </h2>
  307: 
  308:           <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-2">
  309:             {scale > 1 && (
  310:               <button
  311:                 onClick={(e) => {
  312:                   e.stopPropagation();
  313:                   reset();
  314:                 }}
  315:                 className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-slate-800 shadow-lg transition-all hover:scale-105"
  316:                 aria-label="Restablecer zoom"
  317:               >
  318:                 <RotateCcw className="h-5 w-5" />
  319:               </button>
  320:             )}
  321: 
  322:             <button
  323:               onClick={(e) => {
  324:                 e.stopPropagation();
  325:                 closeModal();
  326:               }}
  327:               className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-slate-800 shadow-lg transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:text-red-600"
  328:               aria-label="Cerrar imagen"
  329:             >
  330:               <X className="h-5 w-5" />
  331:             </button>
  332:           </div>
  333:         </div>
  334:         {normalizedMedia.length > 1 && (
  335:           <div className="absolute bottom-14 left-1/2 z-40 flex max-w-[88vw] -translate-x-1/2 gap-2 overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl bg-black/25 p-2 backdrop-blur-md lg:bottom-6 lg:left-4 lg:top-20 lg:max-w-none lg:translate-x-0 lg:flex-col lg:overflow-y-auto lg:overflow-x-hidden">
  336:             {normalizedMedia.map((item, index) => (
  337:               <button
  338:                 key={item.id}
  339:                 type="button"
  340:                 onMouseEnter={() => {
  341:                   if (activeIndex !== index) {
  342:                     setActiveIndex(index);
  343:                     reset();
  344:                   }
  345:                 }}
  346:                 onClick={() => {
  347:                   setActiveIndex(index);
  348:                   reset();
  349:                 }}
  350:                 className={[
  351:                   "overflow-hidden rounded-xl border transition-all h-10 w-10 md:h-12 md:w-12 lg:h-20 lg:w-20",
  352:                   activeIndex === index
  353:                     ? "border-cyan-400 ring-2 ring-cyan-400/30 scale-105"
  354:                     : "border-white/10 opacity-70 hover:opacity-100",
  355:                 ].join(" ")}
  356:               >
  357:                 <img
  358:                   src={item.thumb || item.src}
  359:                   alt={item.alt}
  360:                   className="h-10 w-10 rounded-xl object-cover md:h-12 md:w-12 lg:h-20 lg:w-20"
  361:                 />
  362:               </button>
  363:             ))}
  364:           </div>
  365:         )}
  366: 
  367:         {hasMany && scale <= 1 && (
  368:           <>
  369:             <button
  370:               type="button"
  371:               onClick={(e) => {
  372:                 e.stopPropagation();
  373:                 goPrev();
  374:               }}
  375:               className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 z-40 hidden h-11 w-11 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-black/35 text-white shadow-xl backdrop-blur-md transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:bg-black/55 md:flex"
  376:               aria-label="Imagen anterior"
  377:             >
  378:               <ChevronLeft className="h-6 w-6" />
  379:             </button>
  380: 
  381:             <button
  382:               type="button"
  383:               onClick={(e) => {
  384:                 e.stopPropagation();
  385:                 goNext();
  386:               }}
  387:               className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 z-40 hidden h-11 w-11 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-black/35 text-white shadow-xl backdrop-blur-md transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:bg-black/55 md:flex"
  388:               aria-label="Imagen siguiente"
  389:             >
  390:               <ChevronRight className="h-6 w-6" />
  391:             </button>
  392: 
  393:             <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-1/2 z-40 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-black/40 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-black text-white backdrop-blur-md">
  394:               {activeIndex + 1} / {normalizedMedia.length}
  395:             </div>
  396:           </>
  397:         )}
  398: 
  399:         <div className="relative flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center lg:pl-24">
  400:           <img
  401:             src={activeMedia.src}
  402:             alt={activeMedia.alt || title}
  403:             className={[
  404:               "max-h-full max-w-full object-contain drop-shadow-2xl transition-transform duration-75 origin-center",
  405:               scale > 1
  406:                 ? "cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing"
  407:                 : "cursor-zoom-in",
  408:             ].join(" ")}
  409:             style={{
  410:               transform: `translate(${pos.x}px, ${pos.y}px) scale(${scale})`,
  411:             }}
  412:             onMouseDown={handleMouseDown}
  413:             draggable={false}
  414:           />
  415:         </div>
  416:       </div>
  417:     </div>
  418:   );
  419: }

FILE: src\shared\config\priceTiers.ts

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: export const PRICE_TIERS = [
    4:   {
    5:     qty: 1,
    6:     key: "price_1" as const,
    7:     label: "1u",
    8:     className: "price-tier-1",
    9:   },
   10:   {
   11:     qty: 3,
   12:     key: "price_3" as const,
   13:     label: "3u",
   14:     className: "price-tier-3",
   15:   },
   16:   {
   17:     qty: 12,
   18:     key: "price_12" as const,
   19:     label: "12u",
   20:     className: "price-tier-12",
   21:   },
   22:   {
   23:     qty: 50,
   24:     key: "price_50" as const,
   25:     label: "50u",
   26:     className: "price-tier-50",
   27:   },
   28:   {
   29:     qty: 100,
   30:     key: "price_100" as const,
   31:     label: "100u",
   32:     className: "price-tier-100",
   33:   },
   34: ] as const;
   35: 
   36: export const VOLUME_PRICE_TIERS = PRICE_TIERS.filter(
   37:   (tier) => tier.key !== "price_1",
   38: );
   39: 
   40: export function getAvailablePriceTiers(product: Product) {
   41:   return VOLUME_PRICE_TIERS.filter((tier) => {
   42:     const value = product[tier.key];
   43:     return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0;
   44:   });
   45: }
   46: 
   47: export function getUnitPriceByQty(product: Product, qty: number): number {
   48:   if (qty >= 100 && product.price_100) return product.price_100;
   49:   if (qty >= 50 && product.price_50) return product.price_50;
   50:   if (qty >= 12 && product.price_12) return product.price_12;
   51:   if (qty >= 3 && product.price_3) return product.price_3;
   52:   return product.price_1 || 0;
   53: }
   54: 
   55: export function getNextTier(product: Product, qty: number) {
   56:   if (qty < 3 && product.price_3) return { qty: 3, price: product.price_3 };
   57:   if (qty < 12 && product.price_12) return { qty: 12, price: product.price_12 };
   58:   if (qty < 50 && product.price_50) return { qty: 50, price: product.price_50 };
   59:   if (qty < 100 && product.price_100)
   60:     return { qty: 100, price: product.price_100 };
   61:   return null;
   62: }

FILE: src\shared\lib\product\getBestProductTier.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: export function getBestProductTier(product: Product) {
    4:   const tiers = [
    5:     { qty: 1, price: Number(product.price_1) },
    6:     { qty: 3, price: Number(product.price_3) },
    7:     { qty: 12, price: Number(product.price_12) },
    8:     { qty: 50, price: Number(product.price_50) },
    9:     { qty: 100, price: Number(product.price_100) },
   10:   ].filter((tier) => tier.price > 0);
   11: 
   12:   if (!tiers.length) return null;
   13: 
   14:   return [...tiers].sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price || a.qty - b.qty)[0];
   15: }

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";

    3: export function getUnitPrice(
    4:   qty:number,
    5:   product:Product
    6: ){
    7: 
    8:   const basePrice=
    9: 
   10:     product.price_offer
   11:     &&
   12:     product.price_offer>0
   13:     &&
   14:     product.price_offer<product.price_1
   15: 
   16:       ? product.price_offer
   17: 
   18:       : product.price_1;
   19: 
   20:   if(
   21:     qty>=100
   22:     &&
   23:     product.price_100
   24:   ){
   25:     return product.price_100;
   26:   }
   27: 
   28:   if(
   29:     qty>=50
   30:     &&
   31:     product.price_50
   32:   ){
   33:     return product.price_50;
   34:   }
   35: 
   36:   if(
   37:     qty>=12
   38:     &&
   39:     product.price_12
   40:   ){
   41:     return product.price_12;
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   if(
   45:     qty>=3
   46:     &&
   47:     product.price_3
   48:   ){
   49:     return product.price_3;
   50:   }
   51: 
   52:   return basePrice||0;
   53: 
   54: }

FILE: src\shared\lib\productMedia.ts
============================================================

    1: import type { Product } from "@/shared/types/product";
    2: 
    3: export type ProductMediaType = "image" | "video";
    4: 
    5: export interface ProductMedia {
    6:   id: string;
    7:   type: ProductMediaType;
    8:   src: string;
    9:   thumb?: string;
   10:   alt: string;
   11:   order: number;
   12: }
   13: 
   14: const cleanMediaUrl = (value: unknown) =>
   15:   typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
   16: 
   17: export function getProductMedia(product: Product): ProductMedia[] {
   18:   const galleryImages = cleanMediaUrl(product.gallery)
   19:     .split("|")
   20:     .map((item) => item.trim())
   21:     .filter(Boolean);
   22: 
   23:   const rawImages = [product.img, ...galleryImages]
   24:     .map(cleanMediaUrl)
   25:     .filter(Boolean);
   26: 
   27:   const uniqueImages = Array.from(new Set(rawImages));
   28: 
   29:   const media = uniqueImages.map((src, index) => ({
   30:     id: `${product.id}-image-${index + 1}`,
   31:     type: "image" as const,
   32:     src,
   33:     thumb: src,
   34:     alt:
   35:       index === 0
   36:         ? `${product.title} imagen principal`
   37:         : `${product.title} imagen ${index + 1}`,
   38:     order: index + 1,
   39:   }));
   40: 
   41:   return media.length
   42:     ? media
   43:     : [
   44:         {
   45:           id: `${product.id}-placeholder`,
   46:           type: "image",
   47:           src: "/placeholder.svg",
   48:           thumb: "/placeholder.svg",
   49:           alt: product.title,
   50:           order: 1,
   51:         },
   52:       ];
   53: }
